\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L025}{25}{}{arcus excentrici utramque sectionem terminat. Pars inferior est arcus XO, superior OVRX.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L026}{}{}{Qua re planeta, cum ab apogeo in epicyclo ad O pervenerit, erit quasi stans et infixus in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L027}{}{}{circulo; tunc enim in lineam epicyclum tangentem, atque ab E exeuntem, incidit, et decre-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L028}{}{}{mentum et incrementum variationis diurnae eius aequationis sunt ut motus medius diurnus;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L029}{}{}{qua re moveri haud apparebit eo usque quo punctum O superaverit et in sectionem inferio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L030}{30}{}{rem inciderit. Motus autem in ecliptica semper in anteriorem partem signorum videbitur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L031}{}{}{donec ad X pervenerit et in alteram tangentem inciderit; tunc rursus stans infixusque erit in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L032}{}{}{circulo. Sed cum de puncto X surrexerit, eius motus in tota sectione superiore epicycli appa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L033}{}{}{rebit ad successionem signorum (\textbf{\textit{c}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0154-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L034}{}{}{\Indent Licet re vera nullus regressus planetae contingat, id tamen evenire nobis videtur, ob}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L035}{35}{}{varia centra eius sphaerarum, atque ob varios eius status in epicyclo. Sol et Luna nunquam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L036}{}{}{regredi videntur quia eorum iter diurnum multis vicibus maius est quam variatio diurna aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L037}{}{}{tionis; et quamquam in propiore parte epicycli moventur, hic motus haud sensibilis est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0154-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L038}{}{p. 99.}{\Indent Saepe motus planetarum observavimus variis epicyclorum locis, ut in horum maxima,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-body-L039}{}{}{media, minima distantia, nec non aliis locis in quibus centrum epicycli in excentrico ab apo-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0154-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Ita appellat anomaliam mediam in excen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_left-L002}{}{}{trico, ut est GMH cum centrum epicycli in H sit. At}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_left-L003}{}{}{iuxta doctrinam Ptolemaei debet esse GE′H ut ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_left-L004}{}{}{figura adnotationis \textbf{\textit{a}} patet (\Name{Schiaparelli}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0154-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (2) Seu potius PQ.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0154-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) Scilicet elongationem a loco apogei in ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_left-L007}{}{}{centrico.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0154-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Plato: « katheto Q ». — Codicis lectio in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_right-L002}{}{}{certa. — Vult arcum dimetientem angulum HET\textsubscript{1} in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_right-L003}{}{}{figura adnotationis \textbf{\textit{a}}; scilicet arcum cuius sinus est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_right-L004}{}{}{perpendicularis T\textsubscript{1}H. In figura codicis T\textsubscript{1} cum T con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_right-L005}{}{}{venit, linea visualis enim est tangens ad epicyclum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_right-L006}{}{}{(\Name{Schiaparelli}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0154-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0154-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (5) Seu potius LX\textsubscript{1}; cfr. adnot. praecedentem.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 9}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0155}{66}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L001}{}{}{geo plus minusve distabat. Ex his observationibus augmentum mediorum motuum in longitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L002}{}{}{dine super motus a Ptolemaeo positos cognovimus, variationem eorum motuum et loca apo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L003}{}{}{georum in excentricis deprehendimus, et haec omnia correximus descripsimusque in tabulis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L004}{}{}{postquam eorum itineri in longitudine adiecimus augmentum in singulis iis motibus repertum.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L005}{5}{}{Aequationes et loca longinquitatum fere ut quae in libro Ptolemaei sunt, reperimus, et sicut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L006}{}{}{erant descripsimus; sed apogeum Iovis, quod saepe ad Lunam metiti sumus, secundum locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L007}{}{}{apparentem Lunae temporibus observationum, apparuit nobis fere 8 gradibus minus quanti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L008}{}{}{tate [apud Ptolemaeum] posita (\textbf{\textit{d}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Motus trium planetarum superiorum in epicyclis [i. e. in anomalia] est quod ex motu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L010}{10}{}{medio Solis remanet si de eo motus medius planetae in longitudine dematur. Cum autem me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L011}{}{}{dius Veneris et Mercurii motus aequalis sit motui medio Solis, eorum anomaliae e tabulis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L012}{}{}{deprehenduntur. Anomaliam Veneris fere 4° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ (1) maiorem anomalia apud Ptolemaeum po-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L013}{}{}{sita invenimus; anomaliam Mercurii fere 1° (2) maiorem; hoc igitur per tempus inter nos et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L014}{}{}{Ptolemaeum transactum divisimus, et quod ita itineri diurno anomaliae utriusque planetae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L015}{15}{}{contingebat, uni diei addidimus. Nos enim, quantum fieri poterat, nullum errorem cognovimus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L016}{}{}{quin eum correximus, licet planetarum motus non ita veraciter ut amborum luminarium cognosci}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L017}{}{}{possint, nam observationes planetarum fiunt cum ipsi alicui stellae fixae coniuncti exstent.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L018}{}{}{Quoniam apogea planetarum eodem motu ac sphaera fixorum siderum moventur, tabulas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L019}{}{}{motuum anomaliae planetarum superiorum atque tabulas itineris duorum inferiorum omittere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L020}{20}{}{potuimus, ut etiam comparationem eorum distantiarum cum Corde Leonis [= \textgreek{α} Leonis] (3) vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L021}{}{}{cum alia stellarum fixarum. Praetermisimus quoque mentionem observationum planetarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L022}{}{}{quas locis memoratis (4) fecimus, ne nimis longa fieret de his rebus tractatio.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Latitudines trium planetarum (5) superiorum, scilicet Saturni, Iovis et Martis, concordan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L024}{}{}{tes fere invenimus cum eo quod in Ptolemaei libro positum est; atque ut ibi erant in tabulis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L025}{25}{p. 100.}{descripsimus. Sed in Veneris et Mercurii latitudinibus magnam discrepantiam invenimus secun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L026}{}{}{dum nostros calculos, quos etiam alio modo, quam qui in libro Ptolemaei describitur, itera-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L027}{}{}{vimus, eo usque quo supputationem cum observationibus concordare vidimus. Fortasse quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L028}{}{}{falsitatis in libro Ptolemaei invenimus, tribuendum est homini qui librum e Graeca lingua}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L029}{}{}{transtulit, aut exemplari quo interpres usus est (6). Deus haec melius scit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-gap030}{30}{}{}
\par\vspace{3.45mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-H30}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L030}{}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XXXII.}}
\par\vspace{3.83mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-H31}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L031}{}{}{\CenterLine{De Arabum, Romanorum, Coptorum et Persarum aeris}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-H32}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L032}{35}{}{\CenterLine{atque de alia in aliam convertenda.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.12mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Nomina mensium Arabum haec sunt: al–muḥarram, ṣafar, rabī‘ prior,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L034}{}{}{rabī‘ posterior, ǵumādà prior, ǵumādà posterior, raǵab, sha‘bān, ramaḍān, shawwāl, dhū}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L035}{}{}{’l-qa‘dah, dhū ’l-ḥiǵǵah. Hi menses aut 30 aut 29 dies complectuntur, dhū ’l–ḥiǵǵah tantum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-body-L036}{}{}{excepto, qui 29 $\qfrac{11}{30}$ dies habet (6).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Plat. 4°.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cod. 2° $\qfrac{1}{2}$, Plato 11° $\qfrac{1}{2}$.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Talis comparatio est in \Name{Theonis} tabulis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (4) I. e. in apogeo, perigeo, media longinqui-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_left-L005}{}{}{tate, aliisque locis excentrici; cfr. parum supra.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cfr. cap. XLVII.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (6) Res ita se habet ut auctor nunc dicit; ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_right-L002}{}{}{bula enim fol. 225,r., omnino cum \textit{Almag.} XIII, 6 (ed.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_right-L003}{}{}{Halma, t. II, p. 412-413) concordat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0155-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (7) Muslimorum annus 354 $\qfrac{11}{30}$ dies complecti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_right-L005}{}{}{tur; eius basis est mensis lunaris synodicus 29\textsuperscript{\textit{d}} 31′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0155-notes_right-L006}{}{}{50″, iam antiquissimis Chaldaeis notus.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0156}{67}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Nomina mensium Romanorum, iuxta incipiendi usum qui apud Graecos et Aegyptios vi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L002}{}{}{get, sunt: aylūl [= September] (1) cum 30 diebus, tishrīn prior cum 31, tishrīn posterior}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L003}{}{}{cum 30, kānūn prior cum 31, kānūn posterior cum 31, subāṭ cum 28 $\qfrac{1}{4}$, — quae quarta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L004}{}{}{pars per tres se subsequentes annos collecta, quarta vice annum bisextilem efficit, ita ut subāṭ}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L005}{5}{}{29 dies tunc habeat, — ādhār cum 31, nīsān cum 30, ayyār cum 31, ḥazīrān cum 30, tam-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L006}{}{}{mūz cum 21, āb [= Augustus] cum 31. Dies igitur anni Romani 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ sunt; anno autem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L007}{}{}{bisextili, cuius subāṭ 29 dies integros habet, Deo volente 366.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Nomina mensium Persarum sunt: farwardīn–māh (2), cuius primus dies \textit{an–nayrūz} (3)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L009}{}{}{appellatur, ardibihisht–māh, khurdādh–māh, tīr–māh, murdādh–māh, shahrīwar–māh, mihr–māh,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L010}{10}{}{ābān-māh — cuius vigesimus sextus dies \textit{al–farwardaǵān} appellatur, quo nomine vocantur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L011}{}{}{decem dies, quorum quinque reliquam partem mensis ābān–māh usque ad 30 efficiunt, et quin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L012}{}{}{que extra menses computantur; decimus (4) sextus dies autem mensis mihr–māh vocatur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L013}{}{}{\textit{al–mihraǵān}; — ādhar–māh, day–māh, bahman–māh, isfandarmadh–māh. Singuli menses 30}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L014}{}{p. 101.}{dies habent, et post ābān–māh 5 dies adduntur extra menses relicti. Dies igitur anni Persici,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L015}{15}{}{Deo volente, 365 sine fractionibus sunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent Nomina mensium Coptorum sunt: tūt [\textgreek{ϑώϑ}], bābah [\textgreek{φαωφί}], atūr [\textgreek{ἀϑύρ}], kiyahk [\textgreek{χοϊάκ}],}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L017}{}{}{ṭūbah [\textgreek{τυβί}], amshīr [\textgreek{μεχίρ}], barmahāt [\textgreek{φαμενώϑ}], barmūdhah [\textgreek{φαρμουϑί}], bashans [\textgreek{παχώμ}],}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L018}{}{}{bawūnəh [\textgreek{παϋνί}], abīb [\textgreek{ἐπιφί}], misrī [\textgreek{μεσορῆ}]. Singuli menses 30 dies habent, et post menses}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L019}{}{}{quinque dies adduntur, \textit{additicii} [\textgreek{ἐπαγόμενα}] appellati. Annus Coptorum 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ dies habet, sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L020}{20}{}{quarto quoque (5) anno 366. Aera Romanorum et Coptorum apud Aegyptios ab Alexandro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L021}{}{}{Macedone incipit, apud Romanos ab annis Alexandri Dhū ’l–qarnayn [i. e. Bicornis]; inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L022}{}{}{haec duo initia 12 anni Aegyptii intercedunt (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Si initium cuiuslibet mensis annorum hegirae scire vis, annos integros hegirae in 354 $\qfrac{11}{30}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L024}{}{}{multiplica; quod exibit vide an fractionem habeat, quam, si dimidio die minor est, neglige,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L025}{25}{}{at si maior, velut diem integrum supputa et diebus inventis adde. Provenient dies ab initio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L026}{}{}{hegirae usque ad finem illius anni; hic numerus \textit{origo} vocatur. Quinque (6) dies origini adde;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L027}{}{}{de summa quoties potes septem deme; residuum, quod necessarie septem vel numerus minor}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L028}{}{}{septem erit, est signum anni sequentis; quo de die dominica dempto, remanet primus dies}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L029}{}{}{mensis al–muḥarram anni nondum perfecti in quo es. — Si alium mensem cupis, signo anni}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L030}{30}{}{dies adde pro mensibus integris iam transactis, duos dies propter mensem et unum diem pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L031}{}{}{pter mensem alterum alterne computans; scilicet tres dies pro altero quoque mense lunari,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L032}{}{}{et duos pro mense qui solus remanet, sume. Quoties potes de hac summa septem deme; resi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-body-L033}{}{}{duum, quod necessarie septem aut minus septem erit, de die dominica proiice (7); exibit pri-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Ceteri Arabum scriptores, ut al-Farghānī,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L002}{}{}{Ḥabash, al-Mas‘ūdī, al-Bīrūnī, Kūshiyār al-Ǵīlī, Abū}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L003}{}{}{’l–ḥasan al-Marrākushī, Ulugh Beg, etc., primum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L004}{}{}{mensem anni Romani (i. e. Byzantini) tishrīn I, sive}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L005}{}{}{Octobrem, faciunt, ut mos communis apud Syros}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L006}{}{}{erat. Noster contra a I Septembris incipit, \textit{indictio-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\textit{num} usum sequens, ut in imperio Byzantino, prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L008}{}{}{sertim Constantinopoli et Antiochiae, mos erat. Cfr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\Name{Abū ’l-faraǵ}, \textit{Historia dynastiarum}, ed. Ṣālḥānī,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L010}{}{}{Beyrūt 1890, p. 98; et \Name{Ideler}, \textit{Handbuch der ma-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L011}{}{}{\textit{them. u. techn. Chronologie}, Berlin 1825-26, t. I,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L012}{}{}{p. 434, 453-454; t. II, p. 359 sq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_left-L013}{}{}{\Indent (2) Vox \textit{māh} Persice « mensem » significat.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Corruptio Arabica vocis Persicae \textit{nawrōz}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L002}{}{}{i. e. « novus dies », quo nomine Persae vetustissimi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L003}{}{}{diem aequinoctii vernalis indicabant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cod. « vicesimus ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (5) Quinto quoque anno, iuxta veterum Roma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L006}{}{}{norum loquendi usum; sed iam chronologici scripto-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L007}{}{}{res, ut Censorinus, Martianus Capella ceterique, sicut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L008}{}{}{recepi scribunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\Indent (6) Nam primus dies aerae hegirae, iuxta astro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L010}{}{}{nomorum usum, quinta feria fuit; iuxta usum civi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L011}{}{}{lem contra feria sexta.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0156-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0156-notes_right-L012}{}{}{\Indent (7) I. e. supputa a die dominica incipiens.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0157}{68}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L001}{}{}{mus dies mensis quaesiti. Hoc calculo tabulae astronomicae et chronologicae supputantur (1);}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L002}{}{}{alias vias ne deligas, quae numerum vero maiorem aut minorem dant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Si initia mensium Romanorum ad aeram Dhū ’l–qarnayn, iuxta incipiendi usum Aegy-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L004}{}{}{ptiorum, scire cupis, annis integris Dhū ’l–qarnayn quartam ipsorum partem (2) adde, et si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L005}{5}{}{cum summa fractionem invenis, fractionem, sive maior sive minor est dimidia parte, omnino}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L006}{}{}{neglige. Partem integram summae in 365 dies multiplica; de producto quoties potes septem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L007}{}{}{deme; quod exibit, septem aut infra septem, erit signum anni. Illud de die dominica proiice,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L008}{}{}{et habebis primum diem mensis aylūl anni incepti in quo es (3). Si fractio dimidiam partem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L009}{}{p. 102.}{aequabat, hic annus bisextilis est; si dimidia parte maior aut minor, [nequaquam]. — Si alium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L010}{10}{}{mensem quam aylūl cupis, signo anni binos vel ternos dies pro singulis mensibus transactis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L011}{}{}{adde, prout mensis 30 aut 31 dies contineat; pro mense subāṭ autem nihil sume, nisi annus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L012}{}{}{bisextilis sit, tunc enim pro subāṭ unus dies est accipiendus. De eo quod exibit quoties potes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L013}{}{}{septem deme; postea, ut supra diximus operans, primum diem mensis quaesiti, Deo volente,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L014}{}{}{invenies.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L015}{15}{}{\Indent Si initia mensium Persarum cognoscere vis, semper tres adde (4) annis integris Yazdaǵird}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L016}{}{}{filii Shahriyār filii Chosrois regis Persarum; summam in 365 multiplica; de producto quoties}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L017}{}{}{potes septem deme; residuum, quod septem vel minus septem erit, de die dominica proiice;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L018}{}{}{numerus exiens erit primus dies mensis farwardīn–māh, scilicet dies an–nayrūz. — Si alium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L019}{}{}{mensem vis, signo anni, cuius an–nayrūz cognovisti, pro singulis mensibus integris praeteritis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L020}{20}{}{binos dies adde, mense ābān–māh excepto pro quo nihil est sumendum (5); de summa quoties}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L021}{}{}{potes septem deme; residuum, ut iam docuimus, de die dominica proiice, et dies ad quem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L022}{}{}{pervenies erit initium mensis quaesiti.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Scias Coptos (\textbf{\textit{b}}) tribus diebus in initio mensis aylūl Graecos Aegypti praecedere, qu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L024}{}{}{contra illos una die quarto quoque anno in chronologia praecurrunt (6). Si vis initia mensium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L025}{25}{}{Coptorum cognoscere, annis integris Dhū ’l–qarnayn semper 6 (7) adde; summam in dies unius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L026}{}{}{anni multiplica; de producto quoties potes septem deme; postea, ut supra diximus operans}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L027}{}{}{cum residuo (8), numerus ad quem pervenies erit primus dies mensis aylūl, sive thōth, intrantis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L028}{}{}{anni. Si alium mensem cupis, signo anni adde binos dies pro singulo mense integro iam tran-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L029}{}{}{sacto; de summa quoties potes septem deme, residuum a die dominica proiice, et exibit ini-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L030}{30}{}{tium mensis quaesiti. Si iam omnes menses praeterierint, post hoc quinque dies proiice, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L031}{}{}{habebis initium sequentis anni; nam quinque dies illi, Deo volente, sunt epagomena quae extra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L032}{}{}{menses numerantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Si Romanorum aeram ab aera hegirae, quod conversio vocatur, discere cupis atque scire}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-body-L034}{}{}{quo Romanorum die, mense et anno post Dhū ’l–qarnayn es, origini Arabicae, quam supra}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Eadem regula apud \Name{al-Bīrūnī}, \textit{Chron.},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L002}{}{}{196 (= 176 versionis). — Aliter \Name{Aboul Hhassan},}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L003}{}{}{cap. VI, p. 85-87, et \Name{Oloug-Beg}, p. 11–12.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (2) Nam si dies huius anni, scilicet 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$, per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L005}{}{}{7 dividantur, residuum 1 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) Ut supra vidimus, auctor aeram Seleucida-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L007}{}{}{rum a primo die mensis aylūl (Septembris) incipit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L008}{}{}{Initium igitur huius aerae (anno 312 a. Chr. n.) se-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L009}{}{}{ptima feria fuit, non secunda ut esse debet si a tish-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L010}{}{}{rīn priore (Octobre) annus inchoetur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L011}{}{}{\Indent (4) Nam aerae Yazdaǵird initium fuit feria}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_left-L012}{}{}{tertia (16 Iunii 632 post Chr. n.).}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (5) Nam post ābān-māh scriptores Muslimi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L002}{}{}{quinque dies additicios sive epagomena ponunt;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L003}{}{}{2 + 5 = 7, qua re mensis sequens ādhar-māh eadem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L004}{}{}{feria incipit ac ābān-māh.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (6) Eo igitur anno Copti tantum duobus diebus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L006}{}{}{Graecos antecedunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (7) Nam primus dies aerae Dhū ’l-qarnayn fuit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L008}{}{}{feria septima, primus aerae Coptorum feria sexta.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L009}{}{}{— Codex 7 habet, Plato 5.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0157-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L010}{}{}{\Indent (8) Cum hoc conveniunt quae Plato habet:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L011}{}{}{« quodque minus 7 remanserit a dominica die pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0157-notes_right-L012}{}{}{« iice ».}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0158}{69}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L001}{}{}{explicavimus, 317 (1) adde, nec non dies anni Arabici vertentis iam transactos; hanc summam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L002}{}{}{per 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ dies divide; provenient anni integri, quibus 932 (2) semper adde, et habebis inte-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L003}{}{p. 103.}{gros annos Dhū ’l–qarnayn. De diebus, qui in divisione praeter annos perfectos remanserint,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L004}{}{}{numerum dierum singulorum mensium, ab aylūl incipiens, deme; habebis ita menses integros;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L005}{5}{}{dies autem qui mensem non perficiunt, sunt dies iam praeteriti ex mense in quo es. Fractiones}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L006}{}{}{residuae non supputantur; sed si fractio fuerit $\qfrac{1}{2}$, annus in quo es, scilicet annus sequens}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L007}{}{}{annos perfectos repertos, bisextilis erit; qua re eius mensi subāṭ 29 dies, volente Deo, tribue.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Si aeram Coptorum veraciter ab aera Romanorum discere vis, annos Dhū ’l–qarnayn}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L009}{}{}{simul ac annum in quo es, etiamsi unus tantum dies ex eo transierit, sume; de iis 287 (3)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L010}{10}{}{deme; residui quartam partem accipe, cuius fractiones, si habeantur, neglige. Si nulla fractio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L011}{}{}{exstat, annus tuus bisextilis est. Ergo, si nulla fractio habetur, de eo quod ex quartis par-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L012}{}{}{tibus collectum tibi provenit, unum diem eo usque quo (4) subāṭ transierit deme; subāṭ autem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L013}{}{}{praeterito (5), iis quartis partibus illum diem adde. Ei quod exibit semper tres dies sunt adii-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L014}{}{}{cendi, quibus Copti Graecos praecedunt in initio mensis aylūl sive thōth. Huic summae etiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L015}{15}{}{dies ab initio aylūl ad tuum diem transactos adde; si hoc 365 superabit, de eo 365 deme,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L016}{}{}{et annis Dhū ’l–qarnayn iam repertis unum annum integrum adde. Si annus est bisextilis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L017}{}{}{mensi subāṭ praeterito 29 dies tribue, et 366 dies de diebus antea repertis deme. Remanent}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L018}{}{}{dies transacti ex anno Coptico vertenti; igitur, a thōt incipiens, pro singulis mensibus 30 dies}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L019}{}{}{deme, ut menses perfectos habeas; quod infra 30 remanebit, erunt dies iam praeteriti e mense}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L020}{20}{}{Coptico in quo es. Ad hanc aeram in canone Theonis motus planetarum reperiuntur, cum iis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L021}{}{}{annis 12 (6) alii addantur, ut ab Alexandri Macedonis morte incipiant; primum mensem au-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L022}{}{}{tem in [Theonis] tabulis descriptum, in numero mensium ne ponas (7).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Si aeram Persarum per aeram hegirae vis, adde originem Arabicam ei quod ex anno}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L024}{}{}{praeteriit, mensibus alterne 30 et 29 dies tribuens; huic summae adde quoque dies praeteritos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L025}{25}{}{ex mense Arabico in quo es, et habebis numerum dierum ab hegirae initio ad diem quaesi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L026}{}{}{tum. De quibus 3624 (8) dies, qui inter hegiram et aeram Yazdaǵird sunt, deme; residuum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L027}{}{}{per 365 divide, et exibunt anni integri a Yazdaǵird mortuo (9). De eo quod infra 365 reman-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L028}{}{}{serit, dies singulorum mensium, a farwardīn–māh incipiens, deme, et dies ad quem pervenies}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L029}{}{p. 104.}{erit ultimus dies transactus mensis Persici vertentis. Si ābān–māh etiam numerare debes, ei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L030}{30}{}{35 dies tribue, ob 5 dies additicios illos qui ei adduntur, id est post eum detrahuntur nec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L031}{}{}{supputantur. Dies autem qui sequitur ultimum anni Persici diem, an–nayrūz est. Haec scito}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L032}{}{}{atque intellige, ut, volente Deo, veritatem invenias.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Si aeram hegirae per aeram Romanorum, secundum Aegyptiorum initium, scire vis, de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L034}{}{}{annis perfectis Dhū ’l–qarnayn 932 (10) deme; residuum in 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ dies multiplica, fractiones}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-body-L035}{35}{}{quoque servans. De diebus repertis 317 deme, et residuo adde dies anni vertentis ab initio}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Sunt dies qui ex anno 933 Dhū ’l-qarnayn}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L002}{}{}{vel Seleucidarum praeterierant cum dies hegirae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L003}{}{}{fuit. Nam al-Battānī initium anni Romani 1 Sept.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L004}{}{}{facit, et diem hegirae 15 Iulii.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (2) Male Plato 933; nam ante annum hegirae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L006}{}{}{tantum 932 anni Seleucidarum iam perfecti erant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato male 387. — Sunt anni inter aeram}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L008}{}{}{Dhū ’l-qarnayn (vel Seleucidarum, iuxta al-Battānī}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L009}{}{}{1 Sept. 312 ante Chr. n. incipientem) et aeram Co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L010}{}{}{ptorum (idest Augusti, cuius initium, iuxta al-Bat-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_left-L011}{}{}{tānī, 29 Aug. 25 ante Chr. n.).}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Pro \ArabicRun{الى} cod. \ArabicRun{الا} « hoc excepto quod » habet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cod. « si subāṭ non praeterierit ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (6) Male Plato 15.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (7) In tabulis Theonis motus Solis, Lunae et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L005}{}{}{quinque planetarum pro initio non pro fine men-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L006}{}{}{sium, dierum, horarum ponuntur; sub primo mense}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L007}{}{}{vel die vel hora igitur nulli sunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (8) Male Plato 3655.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\Indent (9) Sic! Pro « a Yazdaǵird rege ». Error apud}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L010}{}{}{Arabes haud rarus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0158-N10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0158-notes_right-L011}{}{}{\Indent (10) Plato 935.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0159}{70}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L001}{}{}{aylūl praeteritos; summa est numerus dierum qui ab hegirae initio ad positum diem transi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L002}{}{}{verunt. Eos per 354 (1) $\qfrac{11}{30}$ divide; exibunt anni perfecti post hegirae initium. Si cum eo quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L003}{}{}{infra unum annum remanserit, sit fractio minor quam $\qfrac{1}{2}$, eam omnino neglige; si maior}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L004}{}{}{quam $\qfrac{1}{2}$, eam velut diem integrum supputa et reliquis diebus adde. Eos postea ab initio men-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L005}{5}{}{sis al–muḥarram proiice, singulis mensibus alterne 30 et 29 dies tribuens; exibunt menses}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L006}{}{}{integri praeteriti ex anno hegirae in quo es. Dies infra unum mensem residui, sunt dies trans-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L007}{}{}{acti ex mense Arabico vertenti.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Si aeram hegirae per aeram Persarum quaeris, annos integros Yazdaǵird in 365 multi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L009}{}{}{plica, et producto dies ab initio mensis farwardīn–māh ad diem constitutam praeteritos adde;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L010}{10}{}{hanc summam et 3624 (2) in unum collige; provenient dies ab hegirae initio transacti, quos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L011}{}{}{in annos Arabicos, ut docuimus, converte.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Si aeram Persarum ex aera Romanorum vis, de annis integris Dhū ’l–qarnayn deme}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L013}{}{}{943 (3); remanent anni quaesiti, quorum quartam partem sume, fractiones, si fuerint, omnino}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L014}{}{}{negligens. Diebus illius quartae partis semper 77 (4) nec non quod ab initio aylūl ad diem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L015}{15}{}{datum transierit, adde; si summa 365 excedit, de ea 365 deme, et annis antea repertis alium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L016}{}{}{annum adde; de diebus residuis dies singulorum mensium, a farwardīn–māh incipiens, iuxta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L017}{}{}{ea quae diximus detrahe. Si fractio, quae cum quarta parte memorata annorum exit, $\qfrac{3}{4}$ sit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L018}{}{p. 105.}{annus erit bisextilis, qua re eius mensi subāṭ 29 dies tribues. — Si scire cupis in quem men-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L019}{}{}{sium Romanorum diem incidat an–nayrūz proximi anni, quartam partem illam cum 77 in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L020}{20}{}{unum collectam deme de 366; de residuo, ab aylūl incipiens, dies singulorum Romanorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L021}{}{}{mensium detrahe; dies ad quem pervenies erit an–nayrūz sive initium anni Persici qui sequi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L022}{}{}{tur annum in quo es. Dies et menses Persarum post an–nayrūz, eo modo quem supra docui-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L023}{}{}{mus deprehendes (\textbf{\textit{c}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Si quid aerae Romanorum iam transierit supputare cupis per aeram Persarum, annos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L025}{25}{}{integros Persarum in 365 dies multiplica; producto adde dies ab initio farwardīn–māh ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L026}{}{}{diem datum transactos; summam per 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ divide; exibunt anni perfecti, quibus 943 (5) an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L027}{}{}{nos adde. Quod provenit sunt anni integri a Dhū ’l–qarnayn. De diebus residuis, dies singu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L028}{}{}{lorum mensium deme, ab aylūl incipiens et fractiones negligens; quod si nulla fractio exstat,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L029}{}{}{annus est bisextilis, eiusque subāṭ 29 dies habet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L030}{30}{}{\Indent Si aeram Romanorum per aeram Coptorum scire cupis, de Coptorum annis, qui sunt anni}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L031}{}{}{Aegyptii a Dhū ’l–qarnayn, 287 (6) deme; quartam partem residui de diebus transactis ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L032}{}{}{anno Coptico in quo es, ab initio thōth ad diem datum, detrahe; ex eo quod remanet deme 3 (7)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L033}{}{}{dies, et residuum de initio aylūl proiice. Invenies ultimum diem transactum mensis Romani}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L034}{}{}{in quo es. Si dies quartae partis superant dies ab initio thōth collectos, de annis Coptorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L035}{35}{}{deme annum, et diebus tuis adde 365; de hac summa dies quartae partis detrahe, et quod rema-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L036}{}{}{net ab initio aylūl, ut supra diximus, proiice. Si in quarta parte fractio habeatur, eam neglige.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L037}{}{}{\Indent Si 12 (8) addis annis integris Coptorum habes annos ab Alexandro Macedone mortuo;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-body-L038}{}{}{quibus si 424 (9) annos Aegyptios adiicis, invenis annos ad quos in libro Ptolemaei reperiuntur}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Plato 365.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Plato 3655.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato 953. — Sunt anni ab aera Seleuci-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_left-L004}{}{}{darum ad aeram Yazdaǵird.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (4) Sunt dies inter initium aerae Yazdaǵird}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_left-L006}{}{}{(16 Iun.) et initium cuiusque anni Dhū ’l-qarnayn}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_left-L007}{}{}{(iuxta al-Battānī 1 Sept.). — Tot dies quot sunt anni}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_left-L008}{}{}{illius quartae partis addi debent, quia Romanorum}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_right-L001}{}{}{annus 365 $\qfrac{1}{4}$ dies complectitur, Persarum 365}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_right-L002}{}{}{tantum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (5) Plato 953.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (6) Plato 387, ut supra.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (7) Sunt dies quibus Copti in incipiendo anno}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_right-L006}{}{}{Romanos praecedunt (29 Aug.—1 Sept.). Plato 30.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (8) Plato 15.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0159-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0159-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (9) Aera enim Philippi Aridaei (i. e. ab Ale-}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0160}{71}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L001}{}{}{motus planetarum, scilicet annos Copticos ab initio regni Bukhtanaṣṣar primi (1), usque ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L002}{}{}{annum in quo es, simul cum diebus vertentis anni. — Nos tabulas aerarum Arabicae et Ro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L003}{}{}{manae descripsimus (2), quibus altera in alteram converti potest; tabulas quoque ad cogno-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L004}{}{}{scenda initia earum mensium posuimus; et harum tabularum omnium usum explicavimus, ut,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L005}{5}{}{si Deus vult, quod necessarium est facile quolibet tempore deprehendi possit.}
\par\vspace{8.34mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-H06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L006}{}{p. 106.}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XXXIII.}}
\par\vspace{4.17mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L007}{10}{}{\CenterLine{De cognoscendis locis mediis et veris Solis in ecliptica (3),}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-H08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L008}{}{}{\CenterLine{ad aeram Romanorum et ad aeram Arabum.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.07mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si locum Solis in ecliptica ad aeram Romanorum scire cupis, annos inte-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L010}{}{}{gros Dhū ’l-qarnayn sume; annum autem in quo es cum iis non numera nisi meridies ultimi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L011}{15}{}{diei subāṭ iam transierit (\textbf{\textit{a}}). In tabula annorum Romanorum collectorum (4), qui cum inter-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L012}{}{}{vallo 20 descripti sunt, in columna numerorum tuos annos quaere; si numerum tuo aequalem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L013}{}{}{non invenis, proxime inferiorem sume. Postea gradus, minuta et secundas ei in columna me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L014}{}{}{dii motus Solis respondentia accipe. Annos autem in tabulis repertos de annis qui tibi prove-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L015}{}{}{nerant deme; residuum quaere in tabula annorum singulorum (5), et quod ei respondet e gra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L016}{20}{}{dibus, minutis et secundis motus medii Solis scribe sub gradibus, minutis et secundis antea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L017}{}{}{inventis. Quo facto, in tabula mensium Romanorum (6), motum medium Solis respondentem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L018}{}{}{ultimo mensi perfecto sume, et sub motibus antea notatis scribe; idemque, in tabula dierum (7),}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L019}{}{}{fac cum diebus transactis mensis Romani in quo es. Postea secundas, quas alias sub aliis scri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L020}{}{}{psisti, in unum collige; de summa [quoties potes] 60 deme; residuum sunt secundae; vices}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L021}{25}{}{autem quibus numerum 60 detraxisti, velut minuta supputa et minutis adde. Quo facto, cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L022}{}{}{minutis ita proventis operare ut cum secundis fecisti; vices quibus 60 detrahere potes, ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L023}{}{}{gradus supputa et gradibus adde. Denique gradus iam scriptos et hos gradus e minutis ortos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L024}{}{p. 107.}{simul collige; si tunc circulum unum vel plures — et circulus 360° complectitur — excedunt,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L025}{}{}{de iis tot vicibus circulum [i. e. 360°] deme quot necesse sunt ut numerus minor quam 360 pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L026}{30}{}{veniat. Summa graduum, minutorum et secundarum quae ex quatuor partibus ita collegisti,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L027}{}{}{est locus Solis in ecliptica secundum eius motum medium ab initio Arietis. De gradibus quo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L028}{}{}{ties potes triginta gradus, qui signum perficiunt, deme; quod infra triginta remanet sunt gra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L029}{}{}{dus, minuta, secundae, quae Sol iam percurrit in signo nondum perfecto.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L030}{}{}{\Indent Si motum medium Solis ad aeram Arabum quaeris, annos hegirae simul cum anno in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L031}{35}{}{quo es sume; numerum aequalem vel proxime inferiorem quaere in tabula motus medii So-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L032}{}{}{lis (8), in columna annorum Arabum collectorum, qui cum intervallo 30 descripti sunt; postea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L033}{}{}{fac ut de annis mensibusque Romanis docuimus. Quod ex summa proveniet, post reiectionem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L034}{}{}{circulorum (9), erit motus medius Solis ad aeram hegirae, sicut eum antea ad annos Romanos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-body-L035}{}{}{inveneras. Similiter motus medios ceteraque quae ad reliquos planetas pertinent cognosces.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_left-L001}{}{}{xandro Macedone mortuo) 424 annos Aegyptios}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_left-L002}{}{}{(365 dierum) incepit post initium aerae Nabonassa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_left-L003}{}{}{ris. — Plato 535.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (1) Hoc nomine Arabes Nabonassarum appellant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (2) Fol. 158,r. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) I. e. de longitudine media et vera Solis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (4) Fol. 186,v.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (5) Fol. 187,r.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (6) Fol. 187,v.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (7) Fol. 188,r.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (8) Fol. 164,v.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0160-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (9) Ut in Almagesto \textgreek{κύκλος}, ita apud al-Bat-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_right-L006}{}{}{tānī « circulus » indicat 360°. Vide supra l. 28 hu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0160-notes_right-L007}{}{}{ius paginae.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0161}{72}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Si apogeum de medio Solis motu demitur, remanet anomalia Solis, quam sub motu medio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L002}{}{}{scribe. Postea in columnis numerorum in tabulis aequationis Solis (1) eam quaere; gradus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L003}{}{}{minuta, secundas ei in columna aequationis Solis respondentia accipe et sub anomalia scribe.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L004}{}{}{Si anomalia Solis, cuius aequationem sumpsisti, minor quam 180° est, aequationem de motu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L005}{5}{}{medio deme; at si 180° superat, aequationem motui medio adde. Exibit locus verus Solis in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L006}{}{}{ecliptica; quem si de initio Arietis proieceris, singulis signis 30° tribuens, invenies locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L007}{}{}{Solis in signo ad quod pervenisti.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L008}{}{}{\Indent Haec aequatio est ad horam meridianam urbis ar–Raqqah eo die quo calculus fit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Si cum anomalia sunt etiam minuta, sume aequationem graduum integrorum; postea, ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L010}{10}{}{differentia inter hanc aequationem et aequationem numeri in tabula statim superioris, accipe}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L011}{}{}{partem eadem proportione qua minuta data ad 60 sunt. Proventum de servata aequatione gra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L012}{}{}{duum deme, si haec maior est, vel ei adde, si est minor; exibit aequatio respondens anomaliae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L013}{}{}{datae. Similiter, Deo volente, fac, si cum anomalia Lunae et quinque planetarum minuta sint.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Apogeum Solis, primo die mensis ādhār anni 1191 a Dhū ’l–qarnayn (2), erat circiter in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L015}{15}{p. 108.}{22° 15′ Geminorum sive 82° 15′ (3) ab Arietis initio. Si locum verum Solis ante vel post hunc}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L016}{}{}{annum determinare cupis, intervallum annorum cognosce, et singulis 66 annis Romanis unum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L017}{}{}{gradum tribue (4); gradus et minuta hoc modo inventa de 82° 15′ (5) deme, si tempus quae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L018}{}{}{situm eo anno est anterius, vel 82° 15′ adde si posterius; exibit apogeum Solis anno quaesito.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L019}{}{}{Apogeum enim eodem motu quo sphaera stellarum fixarum volvitur, movetur, quem obser-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L020}{20}{}{vationibus invenimus unius gradus esse in 66 annis Romanis vel in 68 circiter annis luna-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L021}{}{}{ribus. — Si calculum ad aeram Arabum ducis, fac ut diximus.}
\par\vspace{5.96mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-H22}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L022}{}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XXXIV.}}
\par\vspace{1.45mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-gap025}{25}{}{}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-1.79mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L023}{}{}{De cognoscendis quolibet Terrae loco horis correctis, quae sunt horae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L024}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}aequinoctiales, et horae mediae etiam appellantur, post meridiem}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L025}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}urbis ar-Raqqah.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L026}{30}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Qualibet regione nunc scire cupis horas correctas, scilicet horas secundum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L027}{}{}{quas in hoc libro motus stellarum reperiuntur, nam in eo stellae determinantur ad meridiem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L028}{}{}{diei, quo calculus fit, in urbe ar–Raqqah. Hic est meridies crastini diei (6); qua re septima}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L029}{}{}{hora temporalis diurna est hora prima diei sequentis, et similiter usque ad occasum Solis, qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L030}{}{}{horam sextam diei efficit, aut usque ad sextam noctis quae est illius diei duodecima; et pa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L031}{35}{}{riter usque ad ortum Solis crastinum et ad novi diei meridiem, quo nychthemerum terminatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-P06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Cognito horarum numero a meridie ad horam datam diei vel noctis, multiplica horas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L033}{}{}{diurnas in tempora horaria diei, quae ex gradu Solis in tuo climate deprehenduntur; horas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L034}{}{}{nocturnas multiplica in tempora horaria noctis, quae per nadir gradus Solis habentur. De eo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L035}{}{}{quod proveniet deme gradus et minuta quae in columna aequationis nychthemerōn, in tabulis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-body-L036}{40}{p. 109.}{ascensionum rectarum (7), sub gradu Solis describuntur; residuum per 15 divide, et exibunt}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Fol. 189,v. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) 1 Mart. 880 post Chr. n.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plat. 85° 15′. — Cfr. adnot. \textbf{\textit{c}} ad cap. XXVII.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (4) Praecessio annua igitur est 54″ 33‴; cfr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-notes_left-L005}{}{}{caput LI.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (5) Plat. 63° 15′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (6) Nam, ut in cap. XXIX vidimus, al-Battānī}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-notes_right-L003}{}{}{diem a meridie incipit, non ab occasu Solis ut Mu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-notes_right-L004}{}{}{slimi (praeter astronomos) solent.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0161-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0161-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (7) Fol. 179,v. et seqq.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0162}{73}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L001}{}{}{horae aequinoctiales mediae pomeridianae, quae e diebus inaequalibus in dies aequales con-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L002}{}{}{versae sunt. Si calculum in urbe ar–Raqqah ducis, hae sunt horae correctae; si in alia urbe,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L003}{}{}{differentiam longitudinis ex tabula longitudinum urbium (1) sume, et per 15 divide; horae et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L004}{}{}{partes unius horae ita repertae sunt horae distantiae [longitudinalis]. Si urbis longitudo su-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L005}{5}{}{perat longitudinem ar-Raqqah, quae 73° 15′ (2) est, horas distantiae de horis aequinoctialibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L006}{}{}{mediis ea in urbe post meridiem repertis deme, urbs enim erit ab oriente ar-Raqqah; at si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L007}{}{}{longitudo urbis minor est, horas distantiae iis horis adde. Exibunt horae aequinoctiales pome-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L008}{}{}{ridianae in urbe ar–Raqqah, scilicet horae correctae. Has in columnis horarum quaere, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L009}{}{}{motus Solis, Lunae et quinque planetarum illis respondentes adde motibus mediis repertis ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L010}{10}{}{tempus meridianum diei quo calculus ducitur (3).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent Si horae datae sunt antemeridianae, de diebus praeteritis ab initio mensis ad diem cal-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L012}{}{}{culi unum diem deme; horas pomeridianas diei qui tuum praecedit sume usque ad horam da-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L013}{}{}{tam, et iis, ut diximus, utere.}
\par\vspace{4.55mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-gap015}{15}{}{}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-1.69mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-H14}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L014}{}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XXXV.}}
\par\vspace{4.08mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-H15}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L015}{}{}{\CenterLine{De supputatione ascendentis et duodecim domorum per horas,}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-H16}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L016}{}{}{\CenterLine{et de inveniendis horis per ascendens.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-0.91mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-gap020}{20}{}{}
\par\vspace{0.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si ascendens et reliquas e duodecim domibus per horas transactas diei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L018}{}{}{vel noctis scire cupis, — licet haec iam summatim exposita sint in capite huius libri de co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L019}{}{}{gnoscendis horis ex altitudine [i. e. cap. XVI] — si tempus est diurnum horas ab ortu Solis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L020}{}{}{si nocturnum horas ab occasu ad horam datam sume. Si horae sunt aequinoctiales, eas in 15}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L021}{25}{}{multiplica; si temporales diurnae, in tempora horaria diei; si temporales nocturnae, in tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L022}{}{p. 110.}{pora horarum noctis. Productum horarum diei adde temporibus ascensionum climatis quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L023}{}{}{gradui Solis respondent; productum horarum noctis adde temporibus ascensionum climatis quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L024}{}{}{nadir gradus Solis respondent. Si summa maior circulo [i. e. 360°] est, de ea 360° deme; si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L025}{}{}{minor, eam ut est serva. Deinde per eam ascendens et medium Caelum inveni via quam in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L026}{30}{}{anteriore libri parte (4) docuimus: idest in tabulis ascensionum climatum quaere numerum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L027}{}{}{repertum, et gradus signorum ei respondens erit gradus ascendentis in eo signo in quod inci-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L028}{}{}{deris. Si autem numerum in tabulis ascensionum rectarum quaesieris, gradus signorum ei re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L029}{}{}{spondens erit gradus medii Caeli. — Ascendente cognito, etiam occidens, quod eius nadir est,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L030}{}{}{notum fit; gradus autem quartae [domus] est nadir paxilli medii Caeli.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L031}{35}{}{\Indent Si ex horis pomeridianis discere cupis ascendens, horas a meridie ad tempus datum nu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L032}{}{}{mera; si aequinoctiales sunt, eas in 15 multiplica; si temporales, horas diurnas in tempora}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L033}{}{}{horaria diei, nocturnas in tempora horaria noctis multiplica. Productum adde ascensionibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L034}{}{}{rectis gradus Solis, et per hanc summam, Deo volente, ascendens et medium Caelum ea via}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L035}{}{}{quam docuimus deprehendes.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L036}{40}{}{\Indent Si duodecim domorum reliquas determinare vis, tempora horaria gradus ascendentis in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-body-L037}{}{}{eo climate duplicata adde ascensionibus quibus ascendens et medium Caelum cognovisti, scilicet}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Fol. 172,v. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Male Plato 75° 15′. Cfr. quoque cap. XLII.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Praecepta similia ad horas aequinoctiales}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-notes_right-L001}{}{}{temporis medii Alexandriae supputandas leguntur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-notes_right-L002}{}{}{in \Name{Theonis} \textit{Canone}, ed. Halma, t. I, p. 37-39.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0162-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0162-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cap. XIII et XVI.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 10}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0163}{74}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L001}{}{}{temporibus ascensionum obliquarum gradus ascendentis (1). Summam inter ascensiones rectas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L002}{}{}{quaere; gradus zodiaci ei respondens erit initium undecimae domus in signo in quod numerus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L003}{}{}{graduum inciderit. Eadem tempora quibus initium undecimae domus cognovisti adde temporibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L004}{}{}{duplicatis quoque gradus ascendentis (2); summam inter ascensiones rectas quaere, et gradum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L005}{5}{}{zodiaci ei respondentem sume, nam est initium duodecimae domus in signo in quod numerus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L006}{}{}{graduum incidit. — Eadem tempora duplicata adde etiam numero per quem initium duode-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L007}{}{}{cimae domus (3) cognovisti; summam inter ascensiones rectas quaere, et quod ei responderit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L008}{}{}{erit gradui ascendentis veraciter aequale.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Eadem tempora duplicata de 60° deme; quod remanet, — et etiam est (4) duplorum tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L010}{10}{}{porum residuum (5), — adde numero quo gradum ascendentis in sphaera recta cognovisti, \textit{et}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L011}{}{p. 111.}{\textit{quem te docui gradui ascendentis aequalem esse} (6); summam inter ascensiones rectas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L012}{}{}{quaere, et gradus zodiaci ei respondens erit initium II domus. Residuum duplorum temporum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L013}{}{}{adde numero [ascensionis rectae] quo initium II domus cognovisti; gradus zodiaci qui in ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L014}{}{}{sionibus rectis huic summae respondet erit initium III domus. Similiter duplorum temporum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L015}{15}{}{residuum adde numero [ascensionis rectae] quo initium III domus cognovisti; ad gradum IV}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L016}{}{}{[domus] (7) ita pervenies (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L017}{}{}{\Indent Harum domorum initiis cognitis, omnia reliqua nota fiunt; initium enim V est nadir ini-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L018}{}{}{tii XI, initium VI nadir initii XII, [initium VII nadir initii I] (8), initium VIII nadir initii II,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L019}{}{}{et initium IX nadir initii III. Vox autem \textit{nadir} (9) significat gradum alteri de diametro oppo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L020}{20}{}{situm, id est 180° ab altero distantem. Si quae diximus feceris, gradus zodiaci in quos initia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L021}{}{}{duodecim domorum omnium incidunt determinaveris.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Ad cognoscendas horas ex ascendente, ascendens datum observa; si inter gradum Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L023}{}{}{et eius nadir reperitur ad successionem signorum, tempus est diurnum; si in spatio a nadir}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L024}{}{}{Solis ad gradum Solis, tempus est nocturnum. Cum diurnum sit, ascensiones gradus Solis deme}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L025}{25}{}{de ascensionibus gradus ascendentis in climate; remanet quod sphaerae caelestis revolutum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L026}{}{}{est ab ortu Solis ad ortum ascendentis. Cum tempus sit nocturnum, tempora ascensionalia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L027}{}{}{nadir gradus Solis deme de temporibus ascensionum gradus ascendentis; remanet id quod cae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L028}{}{}{lestis sphaerae revolutum est ab occasu Solis ad ascendentis ortum. — Si tempus est diurnum,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L029}{}{}{arcum revolutum sphaerae per tempora horaria diei divide; quotiens horas integras indicabit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L030}{30}{}{residuum fractiones horae. Si tempus est nocturnum, arcum sphaerae revolutum divide per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L031}{}{}{tempora horaria noctis; exibunt horae temporales nocturnae iam praeteritae. Si contra illum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-body-L032}{}{}{arcum per 15 diviseris, habebis horas aequinoctiales.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Quae sunt ascensio recta medii Caeli, ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L002}{}{}{p. 73, l. 31-35, dixit; ascensiones rectas enim ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L003}{}{}{initio Capricorni numerat (cap. V, p. 14, l. 10-12,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L004}{}{}{ibique adn. \textbf{\textit{b}}). Praeceptum ergo est: tempora ho-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L005}{}{}{raria (i. e. longitudinem horae temporalis) ascenden-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L006}{}{}{tis adde duplicata ascensioni rectae medii Caeli.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (2) I. e. ascensionem rectam quae initio un-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L008}{}{}{decimae domus respondet adde iisdem temporibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L009}{}{}{horariis duplicatis ascendentis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L010}{}{}{\Indent (3) I. e. ascensioni rectae initii duodecimae do-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_left-L011}{}{}{mus.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Scilicet appellatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (5) Est longitudo horae temporalis nocturnae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (6) Deest apud Platonem. Interpretandum: et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L004}{}{}{quem vidimus ascensioni rectae ascendentis aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L005}{}{}{lem esse.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (7) Plato: « ad angulum Terrae ». Initium enim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L007}{}{}{quartae domus apud Arabes « paxillus Terrae » (\textgreek{ὑπό}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\textgreek{γειον}) appellatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\Indent (8) Deest quoque apud Plat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0163-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L010}{}{}{\Indent (9) Arabice \textit{naẓīr}; vulgus in multis Arabicis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0163-notes_right-L011}{}{}{regionibus litteram \textit{ẓ} ut \textit{ḍ} efferebat et effert.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0164}{75}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace*{\dimexpr 7.30mm-\baselineskip\relax}\prevdepth=0pt
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-H01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L001}{}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XXXVI.}}
\par\vspace{2.97mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-H02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L002}{}{}{\CenterLine{De loco vero Lunae in ecliptica cognoscendo.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-2.19mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-gap005}{5}{}{}
\par\vspace{0.30mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si locum verum Lunae, ut in ecliptica apparet (1), quocumque tempore}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L004}{}{}{scire vis, motum medium et anomaliam Lunae die atque hora datis eo modo quem tibi ostendi (2)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L005}{}{p. 112.}{ex quatuor partibus collige; item horas correctas (3) et motum medium Solis ad idem tempus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L006}{}{}{cognosce. Postea motum Solis medium de medio motu Lunae deme, et residuum duplica; habebis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L007}{10}{}{duplam elongationem, de qua, si circulum [i. e. 360°] excedat, 360° deme. Residuum in columnis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L008}{}{}{numerorum ex tabulis aequationis Lunae (4) quaere; quod ei respondet in tertia (5) columna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L009}{}{}{et ex minutis quartae columnae sume, atque suo loco pone; id est quod e tertia columna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L010}{}{}{provenit, cum sit aequatio anomaliae, sub anomalia scribe; minuta autem quartae, quae sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L011}{}{}{portiones inaequalitatis, seorsum pone. Si dupla elongatio, per quam cognovisti aequationem,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L012}{15}{}{minor est 180 gradibus, aequationem anomaliae nunc inventam adde anomaliae; si autem 180°}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L013}{}{}{superat, aequationem de anomalia deme. Provenit anomalia Lunae aequata, cuius numerum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L014}{}{}{in iisdem aequationis Lunae tabulis quaere; quod ei respondet in quinta columna, minima}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L015}{}{}{longinquitate inscripta, pone sub minutis quartae columnae. Postea etiam aequationem sim-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L016}{}{}{plicem quae in secunda columna illi anomaliae aequatae respondet, seorsum scribe. Minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L017}{20}{}{quartae columnae vide quae pars sint ex 60, scilicet utrum dimidia an quarta an tertia an}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L018}{}{}{alia, et ex eo quod in quinta columna reperisti partem proportionalem sume, eandemque sem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L019}{}{}{per adde aequationi simplici iam repertae. Exibit aequatio composita, quam medio motui Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L020}{}{}{nae adde, si anomalia aequata etiam uno minuto tantum 180° superat, vel de eo minue, si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L021}{}{}{anomalia aequata minor 180 gradibus est. Proveniet locus verus Lunae, qui in ecliptica ap-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L022}{25}{}{paret. Eum ab initio Arietis, ut iam diximus, proiice, et invenies gradus minutaque signi in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L023}{}{}{quod, Deo volente, locus ille incidit (6).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Si anomalia aequata Lunae minor est 180 gradibus, Luna in itinere crescit; si autem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L025}{}{}{180° superat, Luna in itinere decrescit. Cum Luna a 0° ad 95° vel a 265° ad 360° est, mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L026}{}{}{tum habet motu medio minorem; a 95° ad 180° et a 180° ad 265° motum motu medio ma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L027}{30}{}{iorem. Soli quoque idem contingit (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\par\vspace{5.27mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-H28}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L028}{}{p. 113.}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XXXVII.}}
\par\vspace{2.99mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-H29}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L029}{}{}{\CenterLine{De cognoscendo loco nodi borealis, qui et caput \textit{al-ǵawzahar} (7)}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-H30}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L030}{35}{}{\CenterLine{vocatur.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{2.89mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L031}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si locum nodi borealis, qui Caput appellatur, scire cupis, eius motum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-body-L032}{}{}{medium tempore constituto cognosce ea via qua motus medios deprehendi diximus (8), et de 360°}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Scilicet longitudinem veram.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cap. XXXIII, pag. 71-72, ubi praecepta ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_left-L003}{}{}{calculum Solis, Lunae et quinque planetarum inser-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_left-L004}{}{}{viunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (3) Sunt horae aequinoctiales temporis medii}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_left-L006}{}{}{ar-Raqqah; cfr. cap. XXXIV, p. 72-73.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (4) Fol. 189,v. et seqq.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (5) Columna numerorum aut columna aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_right-L002}{}{}{tionis Solis non computatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (6) Sunt praecepta \Name{Ptolemaei}, \textit{Almag.} V, 8}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_right-L004}{}{}{(ed. Halma, t. I, p. 318-319).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (7) Vox e Persico \textit{gawzahr} \ArabicRun{گوزهر} corrupta;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_right-L006}{}{}{aeque orbem Lunae ac ambos eius nodos significat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0164-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0164-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\Indent (8) Cap. XXXIII, p. 71-72.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0165}{76}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L001}{}{}{semper deme. Residuum est locus nodi borealis in ecliptica; eum ab Arietis initio, solito more,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L002}{}{}{proiice, [ut habeas gradus minutaque signi in quod nodus incidit]. — Locus nodi australis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L003}{}{}{qui Cauda appellatur, de diametro opponitur loco nodi borealis, cuius nadir est (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\par\vspace{4.65mm}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-gap005}{5}{}{}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\par\vspace{-0.77mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-H04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L004}{}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XXXVIII.}}
\par\vspace{4.14mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-H05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L005}{}{}{\CenterLine{De latitudine Lunae et de eius plaga respectu eclipticae.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.08mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L006}{10}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si latitudinem Lunae, quae est distantia Lunae ab ecliptica, scire vis, lo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L007}{}{}{cum correctum Capitis de loco vero correcto Lunae deme; remanet argumentum latitudinis.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L008}{}{}{Vel, si mavis, loco vero Lunae adde motum medium Capitis; et si summa 360° superat, de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L009}{}{}{ea deme 360°; exibit argumentum latitudinis. Hoc in columnis numerorum e tabulis aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L010}{}{}{tionis Lunae (1) quaere; quod ei in sexta columna (2) respondet est latitudo Lunae eo tempore.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L011}{15}{}{Id si calculo scire mavis, sinum argumenti latitudinis in 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 13′ (3), quae sunt sinus totius lati-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L012}{}{}{tudinis, multiplica; productum per semidiametrum divide; quod exibit in arcum converte; arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L013}{}{}{erit latitudo Lunae (\textbf{\textit{a}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L014}{}{p. 114.}{\Indent Latitudine quocumque modo cognita, si argumentum latitudinis a 0° ad 180° est, latitudo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L015}{}{}{habetur borealis; si a 180° ad 360°, latitudo fit australis. Et si cognoscere cupis utrum Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L016}{20}{}{in plaga sua ascendat an descendat, argumentum latitudinis observa; nam si a 0° ad 90° fue-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L017}{}{}{rit, Luna in latitudinem crescet et ad septentriones versus ascendet; si a 90° ad 180°, Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L018}{}{}{in latitudinem decrescet et a septentrionibus descendet; si a 180° ad 270° (4), Luna crescet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L019}{}{}{in latitudinem et in meridiem versus (5) descendet; denique si a 270° (6) ad 360°, Luna in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L020}{}{}{latitudinem decrescet et de meridie ascendet. Id est, Luna, cum a Capite recesserit, erit bo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L021}{25}{}{realis usque quo ad Caudam pervenerit; cum Caudam superaverit, erit australis usque quo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L022}{}{}{ad Caput redierit. Nam a nodo Capitis in septentriones versus, a nodo Caudae in meridiem,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L023}{}{}{Deo volente, proficiscitur.}
\par\vspace{8.28mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-H24}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L024}{30}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XXXIX.}}
\par\vspace{3.68mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-H25}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L025}{}{}{\CenterLine{De causa parallaxis Lunae et de eius cognitione per calculum}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-H26}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L026}{}{}{\CenterLine{et per tabulas.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.13mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L027}{35}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Parallaxis Lunae est quantitas qua locus Lunae observationibus inventus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L028}{}{}{differt a loco quo ipsa veraciter est, ut calculis determinatur. Cum Luna sit propior Terrae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L029}{}{}{[quam cetera sidera], magnitudo Terrae respectu sphaerae lunaris maior est quam respectu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L030}{}{}{sphaerarum reliquorum astrorum, donec ad eclipticam perveniatur, cuius respectu Terra quasi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L031}{}{}{punctum apparet. Et quoniam centrum Terrae est etiam eclipticae centrum atque locus verae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-body-L032}{40}{}{visionis, visus variat secundum differentiam inter centrum et superficiem Terrae, quae est}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Fol. 189,v. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Nam columna numerorum aut aequationis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-notes_left-L003}{}{}{Solis non computatur. Aliis locis contra binae nu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-notes_left-L004}{}{}{merorum series pro duabus columnis habentur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (3) Male Plato 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 30′; nam in tabulis 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 13′ 46″}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-notes_right-L001}{}{}{est sinus 5° 0′, scilicet maximae latitudinis Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-notes_right-L002}{}{}{(cfr. initium cap. XXX, p. 50, et Ptolemaeum).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (4) Plato 570°.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cod.: « de meridie ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0165-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0165-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (6) Plato: « a 500 et septimo ».}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0166}{77}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L001}{}{}{locus unde oculi aspiciunt; haec differentia manifeste potest quantitatem semidiametri terrestris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L002}{}{}{attingere. Itaque diversitas aspectus [i. e. parallaxis] Lunae maior et sensibilior est quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L003}{}{}{reliquorum siderum, duabusque causis oritur; quarum altera est variatio distantiae lunaris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L004}{}{}{a Terra; altera variatio distantiae Lunae a zenith, quae supputatur in circulo altitudinis per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L005}{5}{}{polum horizontis, qui zenith est, per Lunam et per (1) horizontem transeunte. Si punctum in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L006}{}{p. 115.}{quo hic circulus eclipticam secat, medium locum tenet inter gradum ascendentem et gradum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L007}{}{}{occidentem, scilicet si ab ascendente 90 gradibus distat, — et hi 90 gradus unum e quatuor}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L008}{}{}{angulis rectis sphaerae perficiunt, — parallaxis nonnisi latitudinem habet, atque longitudine}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L009}{}{}{caret. Hoc autem in linea medii Caeli contingere nequit, nisi in duobus punctis eclipticae quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L010}{10}{}{solstitia vocantur, idest in initio Cancri et in initio Capricorni; nam si eorum quodque lineam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L011}{}{}{medii Caeli occupat, punctorum aequinoctialium, quae sunt initium Arietis et initium Librae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L012}{}{}{alterum in horizonte orientali, alterum in occidentali in qualibet regione Terrae exstat; qua}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L013}{}{}{re eorum quatuor angulorum quisque rectus est et 90° complectitur. Reliqui autem eclipticae}
