\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L014}{}{}{gradus, quando in lineam medii Caeli incidunt, ab horizonte haud pariter distant, sed magis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L015}{15}{}{aut minus quam 90°; qua re anguli crescendo aut decrescendo variant. Si lineam medii Caeli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L016}{}{}{occupat aliquis graduum eclipticae ab initio Cancri ad finem Sagittarii, locus medius inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L017}{}{}{gradum ascendentem et gradum occidentem eclipticae in ceteris (2) climatibus apparet (3) a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L018}{}{}{linea medii Caeli in occidentem versus deflectens; ab initio autem Capricorni ad finem Gemi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L019}{}{}{norum, locus medius ille ad orientem versus deflectit: et in ambabus his deflexionibus parallaxis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L020}{20}{}{in linea medii Caeli fit secundum longitudinem et latitudinem. Similiter in reliquis caelestis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L021}{}{}{sphaerae plagis evenit, quando gradus datus plus vel minus 90 gradibus ab ascendente distat;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L022}{}{}{tunc enim angulus minor recto habetur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0166-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Ratio parallaxis latitudinis ad parallaxim longitudinis est ut ratio sinus anguli ad eius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L024}{}{}{cosinum (\textbf{\textit{a}}); summa autem quadratorum ambarum parallaxium aequatur quadrato parallaxis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L025}{25}{}{quae in circulo altitudinis est inter zenith et Lunam. Parallaxis semper in eam latitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L026}{}{}{plagam declinat in qua est gradus [eclipticae] quem circulus ille secat, cum is sit circulus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L027}{}{}{medii Caeli a zenith (\textbf{\textit{b}}); deflexio autem in longitudinem fit ad horizontis plagam ad quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L028}{}{}{gradus Lunae tendit, scilicet in horizontem orientalem aut occidentalem versus prout Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L029}{}{}{alterutri magis appropinquat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0166-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L030}{30}{}{\Indent Ad cognoscendam hanc parallaxim praesertim eclipses Solis adstringunt; eas enim cogno-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L031}{}{}{scere non potes nisi quantitatem parallaxis in omnibus horizontis locis scias. In eclipsibus luna-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L032}{}{p. 116.}{ribus autem hoc necessarium non est, quia ob Lunam non eveniunt. Parallaxis etiam est in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L033}{}{}{Venere et Mercurio sensibilis, sed magis in Mercurio ob eius proximitatem Lunae, praesertim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L034}{}{}{in perigeo, quo tempore eius parallaxis aequat parallaxim Lunae in apogeo (\textbf{\textit{c}}). In Sole non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L035}{35}{}{fit ut in his tribus planetis memoratis sensibilis; secundum Ptolemaeum (4) est ut ratio unius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L036}{}{}{ad 1210 illos, quos posuit communem longinquitatem Solis a centro Terrae. Qua re locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-body-L037}{}{}{apparentem Solis (5) ut locum verum reperimus; nam parallaxis solaris iam tempore obser-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0166-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Exspectandum potius erat « usque ad »; sed}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_left-L002}{}{}{auctor semper ita scribit. Cfr. pag. 12, l. 12-13, at-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_left-L003}{}{}{que cap. XLI, XLIII et XLIV.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0166-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (2) Intelligendum videtur: « in nostris clima-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_left-L005}{}{}{« tibus ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0166-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (3) Ita (\ArabicRun{يظهر}) legendum puto pro \ArabicRun{يطلع} « ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_left-L007}{}{}{« dit » codicis. Plato: « erit ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0166-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\Indent (4) \textit{Almag.} V, 17 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 349), ne-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_right-L001}{}{}{glectis, utpote minimis, variationibus distantiae So-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_right-L002}{}{}{lis a Terra, statuit distantiam geocentricam Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_right-L003}{}{}{1210 semidiametros terrestres complecti; cum se-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_right-L004}{}{}{midiametrus 60 partium sumatur, 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} per 1210 di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_right-L005}{}{}{visae efficiunt 0\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 2′ 58″,51 quae sunt sinus parallaxis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_right-L006}{}{}{Solis. Parallaxis ipsa igitur 0\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 2′ 51″, seu 3′ fere; quo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_right-L007}{}{}{numero Ptolemaeus, Theon et Arabes utuntur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0166-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0166-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cod. Lunae.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0167}{78}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L001}{}{}{vationis in calculum Solis ingressa est, cum terminus eclipticae eiusque declinatio tantum per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L002}{}{}{observationes Solis cogniti sint (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Ex iis quae supra diximus patet locum apogeum excentrici Lunae 60 partibus a centro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L004}{}{}{Terrae distare; si igitur semidiametrus Terrae 1\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} ponatur, Luna tunc a superficie Terrae 59\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L005}{5}{}{aberit; et hac ratione illae 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{4}$ semidiametri epicycli fiunt 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{6}$, atque diametrus tota epi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L006}{}{}{cycli 10\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{3}$ complectitur. Si igitur centrum epicycli tenet apogeum excentrici, ut mediarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L007}{}{}{syzygiarum temporibus evenit, Luna autem apogeum epicycli occupat, maxima distantia Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L008}{}{}{nae a Terra 64\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 10′ attingit, quae primus terminus habetur; exstante vero Luna in parte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L009}{}{}{inferiore epicycli, distantia fit 53\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 50′ (2), quae sunt secundus terminus. Centrum epicycli, cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L010}{10}{}{perigeum excentrici occupat, vidimus a centro Terrae 39\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 22′ (3) distare, quae, secundum illam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L011}{}{}{rationem, 38\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 43′ (4) fiunt; et hoc evenit in quadraturis mensis quae ab utroque plenilunii (5)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L012}{}{}{latere sunt. Si eo tempore Luna supremam epicycli partem tenet, a Terra 43\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 53′ (6) distat,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L013}{}{}{quae sunt tertius terminus; si perigeum epicycli occupat, a Terra 33\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 33′ (7) abest, quae sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L014}{}{}{quartus terminus. Inter hos quatuor terminos distantiae variant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L015}{15}{}{\Indent Si longinquitatem Lunae a Terra scire vis, anomaliam aequatam Lunae accipe; si minor}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L016}{}{}{quam 180° est, eam tene; si 180° superat, eam de 360° deme, et residuo utere. Si numerus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L017}{}{}{anomaliae, quem retinendum praecepi, est minor 90 gradibus, eius sinum eiusque cosinum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L018}{}{p. 117.}{in 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 15′, quae sunt semidiametrus epicycli, multiplica; postea ambo producta per semidiame-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L019}{}{}{trum divide. Ei quod e cosinu provenit, 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} adde; quadrato huius summae adiice quadratum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L020}{20}{}{eius quod e sinu exit; denique radicem huius numeri sume. — Si autem numerus anomaliae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L021}{}{}{quem retinendum praecepi, 90° excedit, de eo 90° deme; sinum et cosinum residui multiplica}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L022}{}{}{in 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 15′, et ambo producta per semidiametrum divide; quod e sinu provenit de 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} deme,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L023}{}{}{et quadratum residui adde quadrato eius quod e cosinu provenit; denique radicem huius sum-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L024}{}{}{mae accipe. — Radix alterutro modo reperta est hypotenusa Lunae, scilicet distantia Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L025}{25}{}{a centro Terrae, tempore mediarum syzygiarum; de singulis huius distantiae gradibus minu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L026}{}{}{tum deme, et residuum erit distantia Lunae a [superficie] Terrae (\textbf{\textit{d}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent Si autem Luna ab alterutra parte inter coniunctionem et oppositionem reperitur, sume}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L028}{}{}{productum minutorum quartae columnae in quintam columnam tabularum aequationis Lunae;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L029}{}{}{hoc est quod te iussi aequationi simplici Lunae addere tempore correctionis (8) (\textbf{\textit{e}}); ei semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L030}{30}{}{adde 5° 1′, quae sunt maxima aequatio simplex. Sinus huius summae est semidiametrus epi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L031}{}{}{cycli declivis (9); \textit{eo utere ut supra iis 5\textsuperscript{p} $\qfrac{1}{4}$; invenies hypotenusam Lunae aequatam ad}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L032}{}{}{\textit{declivitatem epicycli} (\textbf{\textit{f}}). Postea sume duplam elongationem, quae inter Solem et Lunam iuxta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L033}{}{}{eorum motus medios evenit; si a 0° ad 180° est, eam tene, si a 180° ad 360° eam de 360°}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L034}{}{}{deme, et residuum serva. Si numerus quem servare praecipio est minor 90 gradibus, eum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L035}{35}{}{de 90° deme, at si maior de eo 90° detrahe; sinum residui voca sinum primum, et sinum eius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L036}{}{}{complementi sinum secundum. Semidiametrum excentrici, quam 49\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 41′ esse vidimus, in se}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-body-L037}{}{}{ipsam multiplica; 2468\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 26′ (10) invenies. Secundum sinum multiplica in 10\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 19′ (11), quae sunt}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) I. e. cum parallaxis Solis negligatur in alti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_left-L002}{}{}{tudinibus meridianis solstitialibus et in obliquitate}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_left-L003}{}{}{eclipticae determinandis, licet, immo quasi necessa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_left-L004}{}{}{rium est, parallaxim solarem quibusdam aliis occa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_left-L005}{}{}{sionibus quoque negligere}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cod. 33\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 50′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_left-L007}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod. 59\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 22′; Plato 39\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 55′. — Cap. XXX, p. 52.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_left-L008}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cod. 48\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 43′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\Indent (5) I. e. loci oppositionis.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (6) Cod. 53\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 53′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (7) Cod. 58\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 53′. — Omnes in terminorum cal-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_right-L003}{}{}{culi ut in \textit{Almag.} V, 17 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 349).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (8) I. e. ad locum verum reperiendum; cfr.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_right-L005}{}{}{cap. XXXVI, p. 75.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (9) I. e. epicycli, cuius centrum non est in apo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_right-L007}{}{}{geo excentrici; cfr. cap. XXX, p. 51, et passim.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N10}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_right-L008}{}{}{\Indent (10) Male Plato 56′ pro 26′, ut etiam infra.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0167-N11}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0167-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\Indent (11) Cod. 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 14′.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0168}{79}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L001}{}{}{quantitas excentricitatis; productum per semidiametrum divide, et quadratum quotientis de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L002}{}{}{2468\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 26′ deme; radix residui est latus aequatum. Postea primum sinum quoque in 10\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 19′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L003}{}{}{multiplica, et per semidiametrum divide; quod exibit, si numerus [duplae elongationis vel eius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L004}{}{p. 118.}{differentiae a 360°] quo operatus es minor est 90 gradibus, lateri aequato adde, at contra de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L005}{5}{}{latere aequato deme si numerus ille 90° excedit. Quod alterutra operatione tibi exit, semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L006}{}{}{de 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} detrahe, et habebis portionem duplae excentricitatis. Id de hypotenusa lunari ad de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L007}{}{}{clivitatem epicycli aequata deme; de singulis gradibus residui minutum detrahe (\textbf{\textit{g}}), remanet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L008}{}{}{distantia Lunae a [superficie] Terrae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Primo modo in hoc capite descripto (1) cognoscitur distantia Solis a Terra, si 2\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 4′ $\qfrac{3}{4}$,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L010}{10}{}{quae sunt excentricitas Solis, pro 5\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} $\qfrac{1}{4}$ semidiametri epicycli lunaris ponantur. Longinquita-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L011}{}{}{tem Solis a Terra ita repertam multiplica in 38\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 46′ 24″ (2); exibit distantia Solis a Terra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L012}{}{}{iuxta illius locum notum calculo in figura pinea (3) memorato (\textbf{\textit{h}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Si parallaxim Lunae in circulo altitudinis scire vis, altitudinem Lunae (4) tempore dato}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L014}{}{}{sume, et eius complementum, quod est distantia zenithalis Lunae; sinum utriusque accipe, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L015}{15}{}{in unum minutum multiplica, ut partes fiant minuta, et ad semidiametrum Terrae, quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L016}{}{}{unius partis facimus, referantur. Minuta sinus altitudinis, ita reperta, de longinquitate Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L017}{}{}{a Terra deme, et habebis longinquitatem aequatam; postea minuta sinus distantiae zenithalis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L018}{}{}{Lunae in 60 multiplica, et productum per longinquitatem aequatam divide. Exibunt minuta,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L019}{}{}{quae in arcum converte; arcus erit parallaxis Lunae in circulo altitudinis, qui per zenith et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L020}{20}{}{per Lunam transit. Hanc parallaxim Ptolemaeus (5) in tabulis ad quatuor terminos Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L021}{}{}{assignatos descripsit; parallaxim autem Solis secundum unam tantum distantiam notavit (6).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Si vis parallaxes lunares in circulo altitudinis cognoscere e tabulis similiter in hoc nostro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L023}{}{}{libro descriptis (7), ut locum eclipticae ubi Luna in longitudine et latitudine apparet per angulos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L024}{}{}{et arcus ecliptica et circulo altitudinis interceptos deprehendas, inveni gradum tunc medium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L025}{25}{}{Caelum obtinentem et gradum in horizonte orientali, scilicet gradum eclipticae ascendentem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L026}{}{}{dum Luna super Terram in dato climate exstat. Similiter gradus eclipticae ab ascendente ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L027}{}{}{medium Caelum, atque a gradu Lunae ad gradum ascendentem cognosce. Altitudinem gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L028}{}{p. 119.}{culminantis eo modo deprehende quem nunc te docebo, licet in anteriore libri parte [cap. XV]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L029}{}{}{iam descriptus sit. Hoc est, si declinatio gradus medii Caeli est borealis, eam de latitudine}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L030}{30}{}{climatis deme; si australis, eam latitudini adde; quod provenit de 90° detrahe; remanet alti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L031}{}{}{tudo gradus medii Caeli. Si declinatio gradus medii Caeli est borealis et maior latitudine re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L032}{}{}{gionis, earum differentiam de 90° deme; remanet altitudo gradus medii Caeli super horizontem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L033}{}{}{borealem. Ita calculus inversus est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L034}{}{}{\Indent His omnibus cognitis, sinum distantiae dati gradus [Lunae] ab ascendente multiplica in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L035}{35}{}{semidiametrum, et per sinum distantiae ascendentis a gradu medii Caeli divide; quod exibit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L036}{}{}{in sinum altitudinis gradus medii Caeli multiplica; productum per semidiametrum divide, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L037}{}{}{quod proveniet in arcum converte; arcus erit altitudo dati gradus eo tempore in horizonte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L038}{}{}{orientali aut occidentali. Postea hanc altitudinem de 90° deme; remanet distantia gradus dati}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L039}{}{}{a zenith, per quam, tabulis utens, invenies magnitudinem parallaxis in circulo altitudinis (\textbf{\textit{i}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-P05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-body-L040}{40}{}{\Indent Si datus gradus ab ascendente 90° distat, angulus rectus est; parallaxis tunc in latitudine}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Est regula adhibenda cum centrum epicy-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-notes_left-L002}{}{}{cli sit in apogeo excentrici; pag. 78, l. 15-26.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Ita perperam codex; Plato 18\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 46′ 50″.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cap. XXX, pag. 54 et 59-61.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (4) Hoc loco altitudo Lunae est quae e centro}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-notes_right-L001}{}{}{Terrae conspicitur, non quae instrumentis reperitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-notes_right-L002}{}{}{(\Name{Schiaparelli}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (5) \textit{Almag.} V, 18 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 358-359).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (6) \textit{Almag.} V, 17 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 348-349).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0168-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0168-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (7) Fol. 197,v.-198,r.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0169}{80}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L001}{}{}{tantum, non in longitudine fit, et parallaxis altitudinis est ipsa parallaxis in latitudine. Si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L002}{}{}{distantia dati gradus ab ascendente est minor 90 gradibus, eam de 90° deme; si maior, de ea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L003}{}{}{90° detrahe; postea ut nunc dicam fac: Sinum alterutrius residui multiplica in sinum altitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L004}{}{}{dinis dati gradus eo tempore; productum per sinum distantiae gradus dati ab ascendente di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L005}{5}{}{vide; quod exibit in semidiametrum multiplica, et novum productum divide per sinum distantiae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L006}{}{}{zenithalis dati gradus; quod proveniet in arcum converte, et arcus erit quantitas anguli lon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L007}{}{}{gitudinis, cuius complementum est angulus latitudinis (\textbf{\textit{k}}). At si altitudo est super horizontem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L008}{}{}{borealem, \textit{res invertitur; nam arcus tibi e tabulis proveniens est tunc angulus latitudi-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L009}{}{}{\textit{nis} (\textbf{\textit{l}}), cuius complementum angulus longitudinis. Sed hoc non evenit in ceteris regionibus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L010}{10}{}{quarum latitudo maior est summa declinationis [Solis] et latitudinis borealis Lunae (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0169-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent Postea distantiam zenithalem dati gradus in quo Luna est, in columna numerorum, cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L012}{}{}{intervallo duorum graduum descriptorum, in tabulis parallaxium Lunae in circulo altitudinis (2)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L013}{}{p. 120.}{quaere; et quod ei respondet in quatuor columnis parallaxium lunarium, scilicet tertia, quarta,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L014}{}{}{quinta sextaque, accipe et separatim scribe. Anomaliam aequatam Lunae sume; si minor 180}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L015}{15}{}{gradibus est, eius dimidiam partem serva; si maior, eam de 360 deme et dimidia residui parte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L016}{}{}{utere. Quod exibit in columna numerorum in his tabulis quaere, et minuta ei respondentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L017}{}{}{in septima et octava columna, epicyclo assignatis, nota; minuta septimae columnae in minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L018}{}{}{ex quarta iam sumpta multiplica; minuta autem octavae in minuta iam e sexta recepta; ambo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L019}{}{}{producta per 60 divide. Postea productum e septima columna proveniens adde ei quod e tertia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L020}{20}{}{transcripseras; minuta denique e multiplicatione octavae orta adiice numeris quintae. — Aut,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L021}{}{}{si mavis, quanta pars ex 60 sint minuta in septima columna reperta vide; partem proportio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L022}{}{}{nalem sume ex eo quod in quarta inveneras, eamque numeris a tertia columna provenientibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L023}{}{}{adde. Postea quanta pars ex 60 sint minuta octavae columnae vide, et ex eo quod in sexta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L024}{}{}{inveneras sume partem proportionalem, quam numeri quintae adiicies. — Quamcumque viam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L025}{25}{}{delegeris ad idem pervenies. — Numeri qui e tertia et quinta columna post operationes prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L026}{}{}{scriptas exeunt, sunt parallaxes Lunae in circulo altitudinis in duobus terminis apogei et pe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L027}{}{}{rigei epicycli; eas serva, earumque differentiam cognosce, nam est parallaxis simul et Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L028}{}{}{et Solis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0169-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L029}{}{}{\Indent Postea elongationem Lunae a Sole, iuxta eorum motus medios, accipe, a gradu medio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L030}{30}{}{Solis vel a gradu ei opposito numerans, prout Luna fuerit alterutri ante vel retro propior,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L031}{}{}{ita ut maxima elongatio nunquam 90° excedat. Quod invenies quaere in columna numerorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L032}{}{}{earundem tabularum, et minuta in nona columna, excentrico assignata, respondentia serva;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L033}{}{}{quanta pars e 60 sint vide, et sume partem proportionalem e differentia inter numeros aequatos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L034}{}{}{tertiae et quintae columnae, quos servare praecepi. Minuta exeuntia semper adde tertiae colu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L035}{35}{}{mnae aequatae quam servasti; summa est parallaxis simul et Solis et Lunae in circulo alti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L036}{}{p. 121.}{tudinis, iuxta Lunae locum et distantiam a Terra. Eam serva. Postea parallaxim Solis quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L037}{}{}{in secunda columna respondet distantiae zenithali dati gradus accipe; minutis et secundis ita}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L038}{}{}{repertis semper adde ipsorum octavam partem (3), ob diversitatem quae distantiae Solis a Terra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L039}{}{}{contigit (\textbf{\textit{m}}); quod exibit serva. Postea anomaliam Solis in tabula correctionis quaere, et mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L040}{40}{}{nuta portionum quae ei in tertia columna (4) respondent vide quanta pars sint e 60; partem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-body-L041}{}{}{proportionalem e 13 secundis quibus parallaxis Solis ab apogeo ad perigeum differt sume, et}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0169-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cfr. infra, pag. 81, l. 17-18.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0169-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Fol. 197,v.-198,r.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0169-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato: « decimae octavae partis quantita-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-notes_left-L004}{}{}{« tem ».}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0169-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Fol. 195,r.; sed est tertia columna si nume-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-notes_right-L002}{}{}{rorum series duae columnae computentur, ut etiam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-notes_right-L003}{}{}{fit in sequenttnus et in pag. 64. Aliter vero in pag. 51-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0169-notes_right-L004}{}{}{-54 et 75-76.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0170}{81}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L001}{}{}{ei quod servasti adde; summa est parallaxis solaris in circulo altitudinis iuxta longinquita-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L002}{}{}{tem Solis a Terra. Eam de parallaxi Solis Lunaeque in circulo altitudinis, paulo ante reperta,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L003}{}{}{deme; remanet parallaxis lunaris in circulo altitudinis, quae in Luna respectu veri loci Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L004}{}{}{apparet. In hanc parallaxim multiplica sinum anguli longitudinis; productum per 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} (1) divide;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L005}{5}{}{proveniet parallaxis gradus Lunae in longitudine. Sinum etiam anguli latitudinis in illam pa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L006}{}{}{rallaxim altitudinis multiplica, et per 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} divide; exibit parallaxis latitudinis (\textbf{\textit{n}}). — Si id aliter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L007}{}{}{scire vis, ex 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}, quae semidiametrum efficiunt, vide quanta pars sint sinus anguli longitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L008}{}{}{et sinus anguli latitudinis, atque partes proportionales e parallaxi Lunae in circulo altitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L009}{}{}{sume; ex angulo longitudinis proveniet parallaxis in longitudine, ex angulo latitudinis paral-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L010}{10}{}{laxis in latitudine.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0170-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L011}{}{}{\Indent His rebus cognitis, parallaxim longitudinis adde loco vero Lunae in ecliptica, si gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L012}{}{}{[longitudinis] Lunae minus quam 90° ab ascendente distat; tunc enim Luna propior est hori-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L013}{}{}{zonti orientali. Sed parallaxim de loco vero deme, si gradus ille magis quam 90° ab ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L014}{}{}{dente reperitur, tunc enim Luna est horizonti occidentali propior. Quod invenies, erit locus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L015}{15}{}{eclipticae ubi Luna in itinere longitudinis apparet. — Parallaxis autem latitudinis est australis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L016}{}{p. 122.}{si, culminante Luna, locus Lunae a meridie zenith exstat; borealis, si locus Lunae a septen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L017}{}{}{trionibus zenith se habet. Est denique semper australis in urbibus quarum latitudo fere sum-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L018}{}{}{mam declinationis Solis et latitudinis borealis Lunae superat (2). Si latitudo vera Lunae et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L019}{}{}{parallaxis latitudinis ab eadem parte sunt, ambas in unum collige; si a duabus diversis par-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L020}{20}{}{tibus, minorem de maiore deme, signum residui observans; quod de summa vel de subductione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L021}{}{}{provenit est latitudo Lunae quae ex observationibus apparet (3).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0170-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Si gradus datus Lunae in alterutrum e duobus horizontibus incidit, manifeste 90 gradi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L023}{}{}{bus a zenith in circulo altitudinis distat. Si eius angulum in horizonte orientali scire cupis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L024}{}{}{declinationem gradus tunc culminantis, si est borealis, de climatis latitudine deme; at si au-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L025}{25}{}{stralis, latitudini adde; provenit climatis latitudo aequata, cuius cosinum in semidiametrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L026}{}{}{multiplica. Productum per sinum arcus eclipticae inter gradum medii Caeli et gradum ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L027}{}{}{dentem, — qui tunc est gradus Lunae datus, si hic in horizonte orientali reperitur, — di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L028}{}{}{vide; quod proveniet in arcum converte, et arcus erit angulus longitudinis. Eum de 90° deme,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L029}{}{}{et habebis angulum latitudinis. Eorum quisque est [angulus] gradus dati in horizonte orientali,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L030}{30}{}{quo [nomine] ascendens intelligo. — Si latitudo climatis est minor declinatione gradus medii}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L031}{}{}{Caeli, haec autem declinatio borealis, \textit{sinum earum differentiae multiplica in semidiame-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L032}{}{}{\textit{trum, et per sinum distantiae ascendentis a medio Caelo divide; quotientem in arcum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L033}{}{}{\textit{converte, et habebis angulum latitudinis} (\textbf{\textit{o}}). Haec iam explicavimus in iis quae de cogni-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L034}{}{}{tione angulorum praemisimus cum declinatio latitudinem climatis excedat. — Si datus gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L035}{35}{}{[Lunae] in horizonte occidentali reperitur, angulum eius nadir, quod tunc est gradus ascen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L036}{}{}{dentis, cognosce eodem modo quo angulus gradus in horizonte orientali deprehenditur; habebis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L037}{}{}{angulum gradus in horizonte occidentali.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0170-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L038}{}{}{\Indent Si datus gradus [longitudinis Lunae] lineam medii Caeli occupat, a zenith distat comple-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L039}{}{p. 123.}{mento altitudinis meridianae; eius angulus tota Terra semper est aequalis, et invenitur ut in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L040}{40}{}{anteriore huius capitis parte docuimus (4). Si eum aliter scire vis, sume distantiam gradus dati}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L041}{}{}{ab initio Arietis vel Librae, prout alterutri fuerit ante vel retro propior, ne 90° excedat;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-body-L042}{}{}{postea sinum declinationis gradus dati multiplica in cosinum distantiae; productum per cosinum}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0170-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Est semidiametrus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0170-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Praeceptum veritati proximum (\Name{Schiapa}-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Name{relli}).}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0170-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cfr. \textit{Almag.} V, 19 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 363).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0170-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0170-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (4) Vide pag. 79.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 11}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0171}{82}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L001}{}{}{declinationis divide, et rursus in semidiametrum multiplica. Quod exibit divide per sinum di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L002}{}{}{stantiae; quotientem converte in arcum, et arcus erit angulus longitudinis. Eum de 90° deme.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L003}{}{}{et habebis angulum latitudinis in medio Caelo, qui est etiam angulus latitudinis prope hori-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L004}{}{}{zontem in linea aequinoctiali (\textbf{\textit{p}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L005}{5}{}{\Indent Isti anguli memorati sunt directio gradus dati in horizonte, si directionem computare in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L006}{}{}{cipias a gradu ascendente vel occidente, — iuxta locum dati gradus, — in medium Caelum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L007}{}{}{versus; nam arcus horizontis inter ortum gradus ascendentis et directionem gradus dati in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L008}{}{}{horizonte est ut quantitas anguli latitudinis (\textbf{\textit{q}}). Parallaxis memorata iis modis veraciter tan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L009}{}{}{tum deprehenditur, cum Luna in ecliptica sit; nam, deflectente Luna a zodiaco in latitudinem,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L010}{10}{}{anguli et arcus variant, et hac de causa maxima parallaxis variatio potest tunc 6 minuta fere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L011}{}{}{attingere. In eclipsibus autem Solis, maxima ob hoc eveniens variatio, et in locis maxime ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L012}{}{}{aequinoctiali circulo distantibus, 1′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ complectitur (1).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Hoc si determinare cupis ut nullus error prodeat, sume longinquitatem zenithalem gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L014}{}{}{[longitudinis] in quo est Luna, angulum latitudinis et angulum longitudinis illius gradus; item}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L015}{15}{}{veram Lunae latitudinem cognosce. Huius sinum in sinum anguli latitudinis et in sinum an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L016}{}{}{guli longitudinis multiplica; ambo producta per semidiametrum divide. Quod ex angulo lati-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L017}{}{}{tudinis provenit converte in arcum; et arcum deme de longinquitate zenithali gradus Lunae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L018}{}{}{si Luna versus zenith est respectu eclipticae, aut adde longinquitati zenithali, si ecliptica ma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L019}{}{}{gis quam Luna zenith appropinquat. Sinum eius quod invenies quadratum fac, cui adde qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L020}{20}{}{dratum eius quod e divisione anguli longitudinis provenerat. Radicem huius summae in arcum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L021}{}{p. 124.}{converte; habebis arcum aequatum distantiae zenithalis Lunae, quo, pro primo arcu longin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L022}{}{}{quitatis zenithalis, utere. Postea quod e divisione anguli longitudinis provenerat rursus sume}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L023}{}{}{et (2) in arcum converte; exibit variatio anguli. Si arcus aequatus minor primo arcu est, va-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L024}{}{}{riationem illam deme de angulo latitudinis et adde angulo longitudinis; sed si arcus aequatus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L025}{25}{}{primum arcum superat, variationem adde angulo latitudinis et deme de angulo longitudinis.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L026}{}{}{Habebis duos angulos aequatos, iisque, pro duobus primis angulis, utere (\textbf{\textit{r}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent Parallaxes lunares e tabulis quas Theon (3), astronomus Alexandrinus, confecit et nos,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L028}{}{}{eum sequentes, in hoc nostro libro (4) descripsimus, cognoscere potes. Posuit enim Theon pa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L029}{}{}{rallaxes longitudinis et latitudinis ad septem climata, quorum maximi dies dimidia hora inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L030}{30}{}{se differunt; easque descripsit ac si, dempta parallaxi Solis de parallaxi Lunae ut in Ptolemaei}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L031}{}{}{libro praecipitur, Luna esset in initio signorum secundum declinationem qua operatus est (5).}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L032}{}{}{In his tabulis adhibuit horas aequinoctiales pertinentes ad gradum Lunae in sua discessione a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L033}{}{}{circulo meridiano. Parallaxes igitur quae his tabulis deprehenduntur, aliter sumuntur in spa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L034}{}{}{tiis proximis [circulo] meridiei (6), quae est linea medii Caeli die et nocte; nec hae quan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L035}{35}{}{titates sunt (7) ut quae per angulos et arcus deprehenduntur, ob varias causas in iis acci-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L036}{}{}{dentes, licet facilius quam illae sumantur (\textbf{\textit{s}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L037}{}{}{\Indent Quo modo his tabulis uti debeas, nunc tibi dicam. Distantiam gradus Lunae a linea medii}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L038}{}{}{Caeli nocte vel die cognosce, unde scias quot horas aequinoctiales complectatur distantia gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-body-L039}{}{}{Lunae a meridie vel media nocte versus plagam orientis aut occidentis in qua sit Luna. Idque}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Iidem numeri qui leguntur in \textit{Almag.} V,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_left-L002}{}{}{19 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 369).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Fortasse aliquid addendum; vide adn. \textbf{\textit{r}}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) \textit{Commentaire de} \Name{Théon} \textit{et Tables ma-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\textit{nuelles, publiées et traduites par Halma}, Paris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_left-L006}{}{}{1822-23, t. II, p. 98–111; de tabularum usu t. I, p. 67-69.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Fol. 198,v. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (5) Obliquitas eclipticae est apud Theonem ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_right-L003}{}{}{apud Ptolemaeum, scilicet 23° 51′; cfr. tabulam de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_right-L004}{}{}{clinationum Solis in \Name{Theone}, ed. Halma, t. I, p. 144.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (6) Cfr. praecepta quae sequuntur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0171-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0171-notes_right-L006}{}{}{\Indent (7) Plato addit: « ita verae ».}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0172}{83}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L001}{}{}{hoc modo deprehenditur: Tempora ascensionum rectarum respondentia gradui medii Caeli deme}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L002}{}{}{de temporibus ascensionum rectarum gradus Lunae, si Luna ab oriente lineae meridianae se}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L003}{}{}{habet; at si ab occidente huius lineae reperitur, tempora ascensionum gradus Lunae de tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L004}{}{}{poribus ascensionalibus medii Caeli deme. Quod invenies per 15 divide; exibunt horae aequi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L005}{5}{}{noctiales distantiae Lunae a linea medii Caeli, in eam partem versus in qua Luna reperitur.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L006}{}{}{Vide postea utrum Luna super Terram an sub Terra sit; idque [facile cognoscitur,] nam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L007}{}{p. 125.}{Luna, si inter gradum occidentem et gradum ascendentem a parte medii Caeli exstat, super}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L008}{}{}{Terram reperitur; si autem a parte contraria, sub Terra. Si eam super Terram invenies, horas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L009}{}{}{distantiae Lunae a medio Caelo quaere in tabula parallaxium tui climatis, in columna hora-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L010}{10}{}{rum illius signi cum quo Luna in horizonte est; et si Luna ab occidente lineae meridianae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L011}{}{}{reperitur, horas pomeridianas delige; si ab oriente, horas ante meridiem. Horae tuae erunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L012}{}{}{minores horis in tabulis descriptis; maiores nequeunt esse, nisi gradus Lunae sub Terra sit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L013}{}{}{Quod horis tuis in columna signi in quo Luna est et in columna signi sequentis respondet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L014}{}{}{sume, minuta longitudinis et latitudinis interpolatione accipiens. Scilicet, exstante fractione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L015}{15}{}{cum hora tua, vide quanta pars unius horae sit haec fractio, et sume partem proportionalem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L016}{}{}{e differentia minutorum quae in tabula respondent horae integrae tuae et horae proxime supe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L017}{}{}{riori; eam partem adde ei quod ad horam integram inveneras, si hoc minus est [quam quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L018}{}{}{ad horam superiorem pertinet], vel de eo deme, si hoc est maius. Vide postea quanta pars e}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L019}{}{}{30 gradibus signi sint gradus quos Luna in signo percurrerit, et sume partem proportionalem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L020}{20}{}{e differentia inter minuta longitudinis et latitudinis ad signum Lunae pertinentia, et minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L021}{}{}{ad signum sequens posita; partem illam adde minutis longitudinis et latitudinis, si minora sunt,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L022}{}{}{vel de iis deme, si maiora. Habebis ita minuta quae respondent gradui Lunae; eaque serva.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0172-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L023}{}{}{\Indent Postea anomaliam Lunae aequatam eo tempore quaere in columnis numerorum, qui cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L024}{}{}{intervallo 6 graduum in tabula correctionis (1) descripti sunt; minuta in quarta columna re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L025}{25}{}{spondentia vide quae pars sint ex 60; partem proportionalem ex minutis longitudinis et lati-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L026}{}{}{tudinis separatim sume, eamque minutis ipsis adde; exibunt minuta ad quartam columnam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L027}{}{}{correcta, eaque serva. — Postea duplam elongationem Solis a Luna iuxta eorum motus me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L028}{}{}{dios, et haec est dupla elongatio quam in [capite de] correctione Lunae [i. e. cap. XXXVI]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L029}{}{p. 126.}{memoravimus, quaere in duabus columnis numerorum eiusdem tabulae correctionis; minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L030}{30}{}{respondentia in quinta columna, [quae excentricus inscribitur] (2), vide quanta pars sint ex 60;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L031}{}{}{partem proportionalem sume e servatis minutis longitudinis et latitudinis ad quartam colu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L032}{}{}{mnam correctis; et utramque partem proportionalem suis minutis (3) adde, ut antea iam fe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L033}{}{}{cisti. Exibunt minuta ad quartam et quintam simul columnam correcta; quae sunt parallaxis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L034}{}{}{Lunae in longitudine et latitudine, iuxta distantiam Lunae a Terra. Hac parallaxi utere.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0172-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L035}{35}{}{\Indent Postea veram latitudinem Lunae et plagam latitudinis deprehende ea via quam in ca-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L036}{}{}{pite de cognoscenda latitudine Lunae (4) ostendimus; item vide in quam plagam parallaxis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L037}{}{}{latitudinis incidat, ex eo quod in columna latitudinis indicatum est. Si latitudo Lunae et paral-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L038}{}{}{laxis lunaris in latitudine in eadem plaga sunt, ambas in unum collige; si in duabus diversis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L039}{}{}{plagis, minorem de maiore deme, et plagam in quam residuum incidit cognosce. Proveniet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L040}{40}{}{latitudo apparens Lunae in plaga Caeli quae tibi exiit. Denique parallaxim longitudinis adde}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-body-L041}{}{}{loco vero Lunae, si Luna ab ascendente minus 90 gradibus distat; sed ab eo deme si amplius}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0172-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Fol. 195,r. Duplex numerorum ordo veluti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-notes_left-L002}{}{}{duae columnae supputatur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0172-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Addidi, Platonem sequens: « qui circulus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-notes_left-L004}{}{}{« egressus intitulatur ».}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0172-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Sunt minuta duarum parallaxium in tabulis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-notes_right-L002}{}{}{f. 198,v.-205,r. descripta.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0172-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0172-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cap. XXXVIII, pag. 76.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0173}{84}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L001}{}{}{abest; locus verus qui ita Lunae in longitudine et latitudine contingit, est locus eclipticae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L002}{}{}{in quo Luna apparet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0173-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Fieri potest ut Luna, prope medium Caelum existens, iuxta observationes una hora, vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L004}{}{}{infra, vel amplius usque ad horam cum fractione, sit ab occidente medii Caeli, sed in orien-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L005}{5}{}{tem versus inclinet in distantia ab ascendente; aut eadem illa quantitate ab oriente medii Caeli}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L006}{}{}{sit, sed in occidentem [ob distantiam ab ascendente] inclinet. Tunc igitur parallaxim longi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L007}{}{}{tudinis tantum, sine latitudine, quaere, — si his tabulis uteris, — in spatiis [utrinque] pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L008}{}{}{ximis medio Caelo, ab ea parte a qua parallaxis longitudinis una hora ante vel post meridiem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L009}{}{}{fit minor parallaxi tempore meridiano, aut secunda hora ante vel post meridiem minor paral-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L010}{10}{}{laxi primae horae ab alterutra parte proximae meridiei; ita ut scias quo loco parallaxis Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L011}{}{}{nae in longitudine nulla fiat. Hoc tantum evenit cum gradus Lunae in nonagesimum gradum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L012}{}{}{ab ascendente incidat. — Quod cum eveniat, et horae distantiae sint ab ea parte in quam minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L013}{}{}{longitudinis desinunt atque prope medium Caelum, minuta longitudinis pertinentia ad tempus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L014}{}{}{meridianum et ad horam sequentem, aut ad hanc horam et ad horam eam subsequentem, —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L015}{15}{p. 127.}{iuxta fractionem horae quae tibi est, — in unum collige; differentia inter numerum ita re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L016}{}{}{pertum et longitudinem primam quae horae integrae respondebat, erit parallaxis iuxta signum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L017}{}{}{in quo Luna reperitur, aut secundum zodiaci signum sequens, prout in alterutrum aut in ambo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L018}{}{}{simul operatio descripta desierit (\textbf{\textit{t}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0173-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent Postea differentiam inter longitudinem ad signum Lunae pertinentem et longitudinem signi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L020}{20}{}{sequentis multiplica in gradus quos Luna in signo suo percurrerit; productum per 30° divide,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L021}{}{}{et quod exibit adde minutis signi Lunae, si haec eo minora sunt, aut de iis deme, si haec sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L022}{}{}{maiora. Quod invenies corrige ad quartam et quintam columnam tabulae correctionis; postea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L023}{}{}{in additione aut subductione de loco vero Lunae fac ut iam supra ostendimus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0173-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L024}{}{}{\Indent Fit interdum ut quod signo Lunae contingit contrarium sit ei quod sequenti signo pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L025}{25}{}{venit, in inclinatione ad alterutrum horizontem versus. Tunc minuta amborum signorum in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L026}{}{}{unum collige, et ex hac summa accipe portionem proportionalem ad partem quae ex 30° sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L027}{}{}{gradus quos Luna in signo suo iam percurrit. Differentiam inter id quod invenies et paralla-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L028}{}{}{xim signi Lunae accipe, et ad quartam quintamque columnam, ut supra docuimus, corrige.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L029}{}{}{Habebis parallaxim Lunae, in sua [Caeli] plaga, in longitudine et latitudine; idque, Deo vo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L030}{30}{}{lente, propius erit veritati si Luna in ecliptica exstiterit.}
\par\vspace{8.28mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0173-H31}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L031}{}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XL.}}
\par\vspace{3.66mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0173-H32}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L032}{35}{}{\CenterLine{De cognoscenda distantia Lunae a Terra per parallaxim lunarem}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0173-H33}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L033}{}{}{\CenterLine{in circulo altitudinis.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.14mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0173-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L034}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si distantiam Lunae a Terra per eius parallaxim in circulo altitudinis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L035}{}{}{sive observatione sive tabulis, scire cupis, parallaxi lunari correctae in longitudine et latitudine}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L036}{40}{}{adde, si eam e tabulis Theonis accipis, $\qfrac{1}{18}$ ipsius (1) (\textbf{\textit{a}}); ambarum parallaxium ita provenien-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L037}{}{}{tium quadrata in unum collige; radix summae est parallaxis Lunae cum Sole in circulo alti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L038}{}{}{tudinis (2). Si contra eam e tabulis parallaxium in circulo altitudinis sumis, de ea parallaxim}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L039}{}{}{solarem ne demas; ita servabis parallaxim Lunae simul ac parallaxim Solis in circulo alti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-body-L040}{}{}{tudinis.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0173-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cod. $\qfrac{1}{88}$.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0173-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0173-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cfr. p. 77, l. 24-25.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0174}{85}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0174-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L001}{}{p. 128.}{\Indent Si parallaxim cognoscere observationibus mavis, ut nunc dicam fac: Altitudinem Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L002}{}{}{in nonagesimo gradu ab ascendente observa quadrante magno vel etiam triquetro, cuius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L003}{}{}{constructio in libro Ptolemaei (1) memoratur, ut certius et accuratius altitudinem cognoscas.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L004}{}{}{Eam serva. Postea locum verum Lunae in ecliptica in longitudine et latitudine deprehende,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L005}{5}{}{a quo etiam declinationem Lunae cognosces via quam in anteriore parte libri [cap. XVIII]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L006}{}{}{docuimus. Declinationem, si borealis est, de latitudine urbis per observationes inventa deme;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L007}{}{}{si australis, latitudini urbis adde; quod exibit de 90° deme, et habebis altitudinem Lunae in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L008}{}{}{medio Caelo; postea ab altitudine meridiana cognosces quae debeat altitudo esse cum Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L009}{}{}{90° ab ascendente distet, sicut in altitudine gradus Lunae (2) explicavimus. Eam compara cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L010}{10}{}{altitudine Lunae in nonagesimo gradu ab ascendente quam observationibus inveneras; id quo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L011}{}{}{altitudo observata minor est altitudine calculis reperta, est parallaxis Lunae cum Sole in cir-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L012}{}{}{culo altitudinis. Haec differentia crescit cum crescit distantia Lunae a zenith; maxima fit igi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L013}{}{}{tur in signis australibus et praesertim in initio Capricorni; nam tunc Luna, latitudine australi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L014}{}{}{praedita, habet declinationem cuius quantitas est summa totius obliquitatis eclipticae cum lati-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L015}{15}{}{tudine Lunae. Si autem latitudo est borealis, declinatio est differentia inter obliquitatem maxi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L016}{}{}{mam et latitudinem lunarem; nam tunc obliquitas et latitudo ab eodem arcu proveniunt (3).}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L017}{}{}{In initio Cancri igitur, quod a parte contraria initio Capricorni reperitur, ob proximitatem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L018}{}{}{Lunae ad zenith, parallaxis parva est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0174-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L019}{}{}{\Indent Parallaxi Lunae et Solis in circulo altitudinis cognita, sume Lunae distantiam apparentem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L020}{20}{}{a zenith, quae est complementum altitudinis apparentis; postea sinum distantiae in semidiame-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L021}{}{}{trum multiplica, et productum divide per sinum parallaxis lunaris, postquam hunc totum in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L022}{}{}{minuta converteris, si gradum superaverit; [quod proveniet erit distantia Lunae a Terra. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L023}{}{}{Vel, si mavis, sume Lunae distantiam veram a zenith; postea sinum distantiae in semidiame-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L024}{}{}{trum multiplica, et productum divide per sinum parallaxis lunaris, postquam hunc totum in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L025}{25}{}{minuta converteris, si gradum superaverit] (4); exibunt partes [sexagesimae], quibus tot minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L026}{}{}{adde quot sunt gradus sinus altitudinis; quod ita proveniet erit distantia Lunae a Terra, iuxta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L027}{}{}{rationem qua semidiametrus Terrae ponitur unius partis (\textbf{\textit{b}}).}
\par\vspace{7.63mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0174-H28}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L028}{30}{p. 129.}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XLI.}}
\par\vspace{2.86mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L029}{}{}{De apparitione Lunae novae in initio et fine mensis; de azimuth loci}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L030}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}ubi Luna in altitudine et depressione apparet; deque figura Lunae}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L031}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}iuxta eius lumen et iuxta duorum cornuum aequalitatem aut defle-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L032}{35}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}xionem ab ecliptica.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.06mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0174-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Inter cognitiones summopere necessarias est scientia apparitionis novae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L034}{}{}{Lunae in initio et fine mensis; apud Arabes enim chronologia et initia mensium ad visionem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L035}{}{}{novae Lunae accommodantur (\textbf{\textit{a}}). Sed eam veraciter deprehendere difficile est plurimis causis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L036}{40}{}{quibus hae praesertim adnumerandae sunt: Lunae perielium et aphelium, perigeum et apo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-body-L037}{}{}{geum, varia latitudo Lunae borealis et australis, parallaxis quacumque urbe apparens in lon-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0174-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) \textit{Almag.} V, 12 (ed. Halma I, 328-332). De-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-notes_left-L002}{}{}{scribitur quoque in cap. LVII.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0174-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cap. XXXIX, p. 79 et 81-82.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0174-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) I. e. in eodem arcu circuli maximi sunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0174-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (4) Periodus tota, praecedenti similis, in codice}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0174-notes_right-L003}{}{}{et apud Platonem deest.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0175}{86}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L001}{}{}{gitudine et latitudine Lunae, brevitas aut longitudo ascensionum et occasuum signorum in cli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L002}{}{}{matibus, denique luminis lunaris abundantia aut paucitas.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0175-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Multi viri igitur aetatis nostrae, qui scientiam apparitionis Lunae novae adipisci voluerant,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L004}{}{}{in plurimos errores lapsi sunt, nec ad veritatem pervenerunt. Existimabant enim declinationem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L005}{5}{}{et latitudinem stellae ab uno eodemque arcu provenire (1); et operabantur ac si parallaxis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L006}{}{}{Lunae non ob variationem in circulo altitudinis, sed e gradibus eclipticae quibus Luna a medio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L007}{}{}{Caelo distat, oriretur; arcus denique in sinus multiplicabant, principiis utentes quae nec obser-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L008}{}{}{vatione nec demonstrationibus geometricis probari possunt (\textbf{\textit{b}}). — Veteres autem apparitione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L009}{}{}{Lunae novae cognoscenda non eguerunt, nam calendarium et calculos annis solaribus accom-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L010}{10}{}{modabant, et initia mensium lunarium sciebant per coniunctionum tempora, quae calculo bene}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L011}{}{}{reperiuntur. Qua re, licet, ut vidimus, multa in hoc argumento consideranda sint, tamen non-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L012}{}{}{nisi generaliter dixerunt Lunam novam non posse [post coniunctionem] spatio minore quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L013}{}{}{nychthemero conspici. Sed si causae apparitionis investigentur, invenitur hoc esse radicem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L014}{}{}{tantum super quam operandum est; nam, licet quantitas apparitionis observationibus reperta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L015}{15}{}{sit proxima illi quae hac via memorata deprehenditur, si quis eam accurate perscrutetur videbit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L016}{}{}{eam non praecise sed modo veritati proximo cognosci posse.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0175-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L017}{}{p. 130.}{\Indent Apparitio novae Lunae, observationibus reperta, bene cognoscitur per quantitates arcuum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L018}{}{}{aequatoris inter Solem et Lunam, cum Sol oritur vel occidit, si arcus isti in uno climatum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L019}{}{}{observentur; scientia ergo huius quantitatis in uno climatum, — et si in uno climate cogno-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L020}{20}{}{scitur etiam in reliquis nota fit, — est argumentum in quo opiniones hominum versantur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L021}{}{}{circa quantitatem arcus apparitionis; quam nos observando invenimus fere 12° ex temporibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L022}{}{}{aequatoris esse. Iam patuit motum Lunae, cum a Sole discesserit, in nychthemero 12° 11′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L023}{}{}{esse (2), medio Solis motu diurno detracto; haec est quantitas elongationis inter Solem et Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L024}{}{}{nam iuxta signorum gradus, quae fere cum quantitate graduum aequatoris per observationes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L025}{25}{}{reperta congruit. Manifeste quantitas horum temporum est circiter $\qfrac{4}{5}$ horae; in hac portione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L026}{}{}{igitur unius horae aequinoctialis invenimus Lunam fere $\qfrac{2}{5}$ gradus Solem praecedere. Ergo,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L027}{}{}{occidente Sole, atque inter eum et Lunam fere 11° $\qfrac{3}{4}$ temporum aequinoctialium exstantibus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L028}{}{}{Luna non occidet nisi ea 12° 11′ (3) perfecerit; et hac ratione arcus medius apparitionis erit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L029}{}{}{11° $\qfrac{3}{4}$ ex aequatoris temporibus, quae sunt ascensiones occasusque signorum in regionibus. Et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L030}{30}{}{si discus totus Lunae 12 gradus complecti ponatur, cum Luna a Sole illis eclipticae gradibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L031}{}{}{distaverit erit pars nitens disci lunaris $\qfrac{4}{5}$ gradus fere. Amplius et minus quantitate nuper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L032}{}{}{memorata recedit Luna a Sole temporibus apparitionis; eius lumen igitur crescit et decrescit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L033}{}{}{secundum distantiam, et Luna ipsa apparet post arcum maiorem aut minorem. Iisdem tempo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L034}{}{}{ribus Luna, ob varium locum quem in epicyclo tenet, ad Terram appropinquat vel ab ea re-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L035}{35}{}{cedit, unde in quantitatibus illis augmentum aut decrementum oriuntur; quapropter non ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L036}{}{}{unum arcum tantum, sed iuxta arcus differentes Luna nova apparet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0175-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L037}{}{}{\Indent Si hoc modo scire vis utrum Luna nova conspiciatur an non, determina locum verum Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L038}{}{}{et Lunae tempore occasus Solis, secunda die post coniunctionem, scilicet [post] nonam et vice-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L039}{}{}{simam diem mensis Arabici; amborum luminarium locum verum in ecliptica qua vis urbe}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L040}{40}{}{cognosce, item veram Lunae latitudinem. Postea parallaxim Lunae in longitudine et latitudine}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L041}{}{}{tempore quo Sol occidit deprehende, ut ex ea scias veraciter locum apparentem Lunae respectu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L042}{}{}{eclipticae in longitudine et latitudine, et plagam latitudinis. His cognitis, declinationem appa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-body-L043}{}{p. 131.}{rentem Lunae et gradum cum Luna culminantem inveni; e quibus arcum semidiurnum Lu-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0175-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) I. e. in eodem arcu circuli maximi nume-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-notes_left-L002}{}{}{rari. — Idem loquendi modus in cap. XL, p. 85.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0175-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) Est motus synodicus Lunae; cfr. cap. XXX.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0175-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0175-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato 15° 11′.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0176}{87}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L001}{}{}{nae, scilicet Lunae dimidiam moram super Terram, deprehende ut in anteriore parte libri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L002}{}{}{explicavimus, in capite de cognitione declinationis stellae et gradus cum ea culminantis per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L003}{}{}{latitudinem stellae et declinationem gradus eclipticae in quo sidus est [cap. XVIII], nec non}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L004}{}{}{in capite de supputando arcu semidiurno stellae per stellae declinationem [cap. XIX].}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0176-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L005}{5}{}{\Indent Arcum semidiurnum Lunae adde temporibus ascensionum rectarum gradus cum ea cul-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L006}{}{}{minantis; exibunt tempora ascensionum nadir gradus cum quo Luna (1) in eo climate occidit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L007}{}{}{De his deme tempora ascensionum gradus gradui Solis oppositi in eo climate; remanet distantia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L008}{}{}{inter Solem et Lunam, expressa gradibus occasuum. Eam serva. Postea gradum verum [lon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L009}{}{}{gitudinis] in quo Luna est, eiusque latitudinem veram cognosce; quadratum differentiae inter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L010}{10}{}{gradus veros Solis et Lunae [in longitudine] adde quadrato latitudinis Lunae; radix huius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L011}{}{}{summae est fere distantia Lunae a Sole. Sed si eam cognoscere malueris per praecepta in ante-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L012}{}{}{riore parte libri exposita in capite de distantiis inter stellas secundum earum positione in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L013}{}{}{sphaera caelesti [cap. XXVI], quod invenies certius et verius erit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0176-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L014}{}{}{\Indent Si Luna amplius vel minus quam 12° 11′ a Sole distat, differentiam sume et vide quanta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L015}{15}{}{pars sit ex iis 12° 11′, quae sunt quantitas luminis Lunae ad apparitionem; partem propor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L016}{}{}{tionalem ex differentia sume, eamque \textit{portionem} appella, ut id habeas quo arcus apparitionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L017}{}{}{crescit vel decrescit. Postea anomaliam Lunae aequatam quaere in tabula correctionis (2), et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L018}{}{}{minuta respondentia in tertia columna, qua partes longinquitatum Lunae continentur, sume. Si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L019}{}{}{haec minuta sunt 30, Luna est in media sua distantia a Terra; tunc, si portio illa (3) decre-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L020}{20}{}{scens est, ipsam adde 11° 45′, quae sunt quantitas arcus apparitionis; si autem portio illa est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L021}{}{}{crescens, ipsam de 11° 45′ (4) deme. Si contra minutorum numerus maior aut minor est quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L022}{}{}{30, differentia a 30 vide quanta pars ex 30 sit, et partem proportionalem sume ex portione illa;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L023}{}{}{eius quod invenies $\qfrac{1}{12}$ accipe, sicut diametrus Lunae respectu diametri mediae fere duodecima}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L024}{}{}{parte mediae diametri crescit et decrescit. Illam duodecimam partem adde portioni illi, si portio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L025}{25}{p. 132.}{est crescens et minuta tertiae columnae 30 superant; si minutorum numerus minor est quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L026}{}{}{30, duodecimam partem illam de portione deme. Si autem portio est decrescens et minuta 30}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L027}{}{}{superant, $\qfrac{1}{12}$ inventam de portione illa deme; at si minutorum numerus est minor quam 30,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L028}{}{}{eam duodecimam partem portioni adde. Portionem ex augmento aut deminutione provenien-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L029}{}{}{tem, si super 12° 11′ crescit, de 11° 45′ deme; si decrescit adde iis 11° 45′. Exibit quantitas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L030}{30}{}{arcus apparitionis aequata ad augmentum aut decrementum luminis lunaris, iuxta longinqui-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L031}{}{}{tatem tunc inter Lunam et Terram intercedentem. — Si gradus occasuum inter Solem et Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L032}{}{}{nam, quos servare praecepi, sunt ut arcus aequatus apparitionis vel eo maiores, Luna nova}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L033}{}{}{visibilis est; sed si arcu superantur, ea in urbe non conspicitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0176-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L034}{}{}{\Indent Aer clarus et purus visionem Lunae novae iuvat, quam contra aer crassus et nubilus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L035}{35}{}{impedit; nec omittenda est varia oculorum acies. Primo crepusculo vespertino aer crassus est,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L036}{}{}{sed postea rarescit ante quam Luna ab horizonte evanescat terminumque sui occasus attingat;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L037}{}{}{ergo tunc Luna nova videbitur post tempus apparitionis calculis repertum. Qua re de Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L038}{}{}{videnda in apparitionis loco non est desperandum, nisi Lunam occidisse et de horizonte aufu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L039}{}{}{gisse iam certe sciatur. Ob eas causas fieri potest ut uno loco et non in alio Luna appareat;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L040}{40}{}{idque etiam evenit propter variam longitudinem vel brevitatem ascensionum occasuumque si-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-body-L041}{}{}{gnorum in diversis Terrae regionibus (\textbf{\textit{c}}).}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0176-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cod. « Sol ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0176-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Fol. 195,r. — Binae series numerorum, qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-notes_left-L003}{}{}{sunt tabulae argumenta, pro duabus columnis sup-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-notes_left-L004}{}{}{putantur,}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0176-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) I. e. portio nuper inventa, qua arcus appa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-notes_right-L002}{}{}{ritionis crescit vel decrescit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0176-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cod. hic et infra: 41° 45′ (\ArabicRun{ما} 41 pro \ArabicRun{ٮا} vel}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0176-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\ArabicRun{يا} 11).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0177}{88}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0177-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Ut nunc ad opinionem revertamur, de cuius veritate non dubitatur secundum ea quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L002}{}{}{veteres de apparitione Lunae novae affirmabant, eam scilicet conspici non posse nisi saltem nych-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L003}{}{}{themerum elapsum sit, si distantiam Lunae a Sole accipimus cum Luna suo minimo motu et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L004}{}{}{Sol suo maximo moventur, idest cum Luna apogeum epicycli, Sol perigeum occupat, inveni-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L005}{5}{}{mus longinquitatem Lunae a Sole in nychthemero 10° $\qfrac{5}{6}$ complecti, quae sunt quantitas arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L006}{}{}{aequatorialis apparitionis iuxta eam rationem. At si Luna maximo suo motu et Sol minimo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L007}{}{}{suo moventur, scilicet occupante Luna perigeum et Sole apogeum epicycli, invenimus elonga-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L008}{}{p. 133.}{tionem in nychthemero 13° $\qfrac{2}{3}$ (1) circiter esse. Hac igitur eclipticae quantitate utemur in lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L009}{}{}{mine Lunae tempore apparitionis; et dicemus, intercedentibus inter Solem et Lunam 10° $\qfrac{5}{6}$ e}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L010}{10}{}{temporibus aequatoris, et elongatione Lunae a Sole 13° $\qfrac{2}{3}$ (2) graduum eclipticae complectente,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L011}{}{}{Lunam procul dubio in loco apparitionis esse, nisi aeris status, ut antea diximus, id impediat.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L012}{}{}{Et quia Luna potest plus minusve quam his eclipticae gradibus memoratis a Sole recedere,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L013}{}{}{atque in epicyclo ab apogeo ad perigeum tendere, quantitas apparitionis potest, ut supra di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L014}{}{}{ximus, variare.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0177-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L015}{15}{}{\Indent Si hac via cupis apparitionem veram cognoscere, Solem et Lunam ad tempus memoratum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L016}{}{}{iis modis quos ostendimus corrige, ut distantiam Lunae a Sole in gradibus occasuum regionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L017}{}{}{cognoscas; postea elongationem Lunae a Sole in gradibus eclipticae iuxta Lunae latitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L018}{}{}{eo modo inveni, et si maior aut minor est quam 13° 40′, differentiam sume et vide quanta pars}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L019}{}{}{sit ex 13° 40′; [de illis 10° 50′] partem proportionalem ipsi differentiae accipe; et haec erit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L020}{20}{}{\textit{portio}. Si Luna est in apogeo [epicycli], in quo erat tempore quantitatis dati arcus apparitionis, —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L021}{}{}{id vero evenit cum anomalia Lunae fere 360° complectitur, — non est [arcus] maior nec}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L022}{}{}{minor [arcu dato] nisi quantitate prorsus insensibili. Portionem illam de 10° 50′ (3) deme, si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L023}{}{}{est crescens; iis 10° 50′ adde, si decrescens; exibit arcus aequatus apparitionis. Cum Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L024}{}{}{iam ab apogeo suo recesserit, eius anomaliam aequatam in tabula correctionis (4) quaere;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L025}{25}{}{minuta in tertia columna respondentia vide quae pars sint ex 60, et partem proportionalem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L026}{}{}{e portione sume; postea $\qfrac{1}{5}$ huius partis proportionalis sumptae accipe, quia $\qfrac{1}{5}$ minimae dia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L027}{}{}{metri lunaris (5) est quantitas qua Lunae maxima diametrus minimam excedit; denique eam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L028}{}{}{quintam partem de portione deme, si portio est ad modum deminutionis, at portioni adde si}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L029}{}{}{portio est ad modum augmenti. Quod provenit converte in aequatoris tempora, et adde 10° 50′,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L030}{30}{}{si portio a 13° 40′ decrescens erat, sed de iis deme, si portio crescens. Proveniet quantitas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L031}{}{}{aequata arcus apparitionis. Si haec est aequalis longinquitati Lunae a Sole per occasuum tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L032}{}{}{pora, vel ea minor, scias Lunam procul dubio esse in loco apparitionis, sive quoddam impe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L033}{}{}{dimentorum memoratorum visum prohibet, sive non. At si arcus aequatus tempora occasuum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L034}{}{}{excedit, scias ea in urbe Lunam novam haud visibilem esse (\textbf{\textit{d}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0177-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L035}{35}{p. 134.}{\Indent Quod autem e portione illa provenit hoc modo in tempora aequinoctialia convertes: tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L036}{}{}{pora, quae diximus esse tempora ascensionalia nadir gradus Lunae in climate, quaere in ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L037}{}{}{bulis; gradus signorum iis respondens est nadir gradus cum quo Luna occidit; eum adde illi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L038}{}{}{quod e portione provenit, si portio ad modum augmenti est; aut de eo quod e portione exit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L039}{}{}{deme, si portio est ad modum deminutionis. Ei quod alterutra operatione invenisti vide quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L040}{40}{}{tempora ascensionalia respondeant, et differentiam inter haec et ea tempora priora, — quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L041}{}{}{sunt tempora ascensionalia nadir gradus Lunae, — cognosce; differentia enim est quantitas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L042}{}{}{portionis, temporibus aequinoctialibus expressa. Id ab arcu apparitionis deme, aut ei adde,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-body-L043}{}{}{prout, Deo volente, opportunum est.}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0177-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Plato 13° 30′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0177-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Plato 13° 30′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0177-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod. hic et infra semper 64° 50′.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0177-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Fol. 195,r.; series numerorum anomaliae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-notes_right-L002}{}{}{sunt, ut saepe monui, duae columnae supputandae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0177-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0177-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cod. « Solis ».}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0178}{89}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0178-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L001}{}{}{\Indent Matutinam apparitionem Lunae in fine mensium eodem modo deprehendes, hoc tamen}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L002}{}{}{excepto quod adhibenda sunt tempora ascensionalia ipsius gradus Solis et ipsius gradus Lunae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L003}{}{}{[non, ut antea, tempora nadir horum graduum]. Tempora ascensionum gradus Lunae cogno-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L004}{}{}{sces, arcu semidiurno Lunae de temporibus ascensionum rectarum gradus cum Luna culmi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L005}{5}{}{nantis demendo; residuum enim sunt tempora ascensionalia gradus cum quo Luna in climate}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L006}{}{}{ascendit. De quibus deme tempora ascensionalia gradus Solis; exibunt tempora ascensionum}
