\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L007}{}{}{quae sunt inter Solem et Lunam, si Luna ab orientis parte reperitur. Si arcus apparitionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L008}{}{}{tibi proveniens aequatur arcui temporum ascensionalium inter Solem et Lunam intercedentium,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L009}{}{}{vel eo minor, Luna matutino tempore ante ortum Solis apparet; si autem eo maior est, Luna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L010}{10}{}{occultatur radiis nec aspicitur. Debes etiam correctionem Solis et Lunae ponere ad tempus or-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L011}{}{}{tus Solis vicesimo octavo (1) die mensis Arabici, qui uno die coniunctionem praecedit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0178-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L012}{}{}{\Indent Si imaginem Lunae novae, ut apparet, scilicet iuxta cornuum aequalitatem aut inclina-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L013}{}{}{tionem, et iuxta luminis quantitatem, effingere vis, gradus eclipticae qui, secundum latitudi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L014}{}{}{nem Lunae, inter Solem et Lunam sunt, divide per 15°, ut proveniant duodecimae (2) partes disci}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L015}{15}{}{lunaris; quae exeunt sunt digiti luminis. Postea, qua vis semidiametro, circulum describe, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L016}{}{}{duabus lineis, se in centro ad angulos rectos mutuo secantibus, in quatuor quadrantes divide.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L017}{}{}{Super extremas lineas cardines horizontis nota; singulos quadrantes in 90° divide; et iuxta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L018}{}{}{veram Lunae latitudinem, a puncto orientis et occidentis in plagam latitudinis Lunae proce-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L019}{}{}{dens duo signa [in circumferentia] pone, ut uterque arcus quantitatem latitudinis Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L020}{20}{p. 135.}{aequet. Deinde latus regulae super duo signa ponens, per ea lineam rectam duc, diametro}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L021}{}{}{circuli parallelam, et extra circumferentiam in orientem versus protrahe quantitate semidia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L022}{}{}{metri circuli. In hac linea erit Lunae transitus in longitudine eo tempore et reliquis tempo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L023}{}{}{ribus, iuxta Lunae latitudinem, ab hora apparitionis ad horam medii luminis. Tunc centrum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L024}{}{}{circuli incidit in locum circumferentiae et lineae communem; sed ab hora medii luminis ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L025}{25}{}{horam pleni luminis, centrum percurret eam lineam a circumferentia in orientem versus exeun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L026}{}{}{tem, usque quo ad extremitatem lineae pervenerit, quae est inter eius circulum et circulum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L027}{}{}{Solis. Primus ille circulus, qui pro Sole descriptus erat, fit igitur circulus Lunae tempore}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L028}{}{}{pleni luminis. Postea in circumferentia a puncto septentrionum versus locum orientis computa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L029}{}{}{eundem numerum qui est inter Solem et Lunam; similiter etiam a meridie in orientem versus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L030}{30}{}{numera. Puncta ad quae ita pervenisti, linea recta coniunge; locus quo duae lineae se invi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L031}{}{}{cem secant est centrum disci lunaris. Circa istud circulum priori aequalem describe; spatium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L032}{}{}{inter duos arcus est Lunae novae imago (\textbf{\textit{e}}). Puncta intersectionis circulorum coniunge linea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L033}{}{}{recta, quae secunda diametrus circuli erit; lineam rectam quoque duc transeuntem per cen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L034}{}{}{trum utriusque circuli et per duos arcus, ita ut Lunam novam in duas partes aequales divi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L035}{35}{}{dat. Per gradus in quos circumferentiam divisisti cognosces etiam quantum utrumque cornu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L036}{}{}{Lunae ab ecliptica declinet; nam tunc terminus eclipticae in horizonte cognoscitur per azi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L037}{}{}{muth arcus horizontis qui eo tempore cum eo ascendit et occidit.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0178-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L038}{}{}{\Indent Circa centrum E et diametros AC, BD, sit discus Solis ABCD; punctum A ponamus di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L039}{}{}{rectionem meridiei, C septentrionum, B orientis, D occidentis. Lunae latitudo borealis 5° com-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L040}{40}{}{plectatur; eius vera elongatio a Sole 12° (3). A punctis B et D versus locum C septentrionum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L041}{}{}{procedentes, de circulo portionem aequalem latitudini Lunae abscindamus, cuius extremitates}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-body-L042}{}{}{sint H et K; postea lineam rectam HK signemus et usque ad L ita producamus, ut sit KL}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0178-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Plato 18.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0178-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Plato 15. — Hemisphaerium visibile Lunae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-notes_left-L003}{}{}{tempore plenilunii omnino collustratum, ponit 12}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-notes_right-L001}{}{}{digitos complecti, quorum quisque est 15 graduum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-notes_right-L002}{}{}{semicircumferentiae disci lunaris.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0178-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0178-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato 15.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 12}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0179}{90}
\par\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.561\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0179-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0179-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.419\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.581\FullSourceWidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L001}{}{}{aequalis EB. Item a pun-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L002}{}{}{ctis A et C, versus B pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L003}{}{}{gredientes, secemus duos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L004}{}{}{arcus, quorum quisque spa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L005}{5}{}{tium inter Solem et Lunam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L006}{}{}{aequet; eorum puncta ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L007}{}{}{trema M et S vocemus, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L008}{}{}{rectam MS ducamus, quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L009}{}{}{lineam HK in F secabit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L010}{10}{}{Circa hoc centrum discum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L011}{}{p. 136.}{Lunae, priori circulo ae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L012}{}{}{qualem, describamus; loca}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L013}{}{}{intersectionis circulorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L014}{}{}{sint P et Q. Lineam EF}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L015}{15}{}{ducamus et protrahamus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L016}{}{}{usque ad punctum T primi}
\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L017}{}{}{circuli, quod in medium arcum PQ incidit; locum quo linea FET circumferentiam disci lunaris}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L018}{}{}{secat, littera G notemus. Linea TG erit igitur locus medius arcus Lunae novae et locus medius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L019}{}{}{luminis Lunae, idest quantitas digitorum lucis lunaris. Puncta P et Q erunt extremitates cor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L020}{20}{}{nuum Lunae novae; eorum inclinatio respectu lineae aequalitatis super eclipticam erectae inno-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L021}{}{}{tescet ex arcubus AP, CQ. Nam tunc D (1) erit in directione gradus occidentis, B in directione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L022}{}{}{gradus orientis e circulo horizontali; itaque BD erit linea dimidiae eclipticae. Hac figura (2)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L023}{}{}{quovis mensis tempore cognosces imaginem luminis Lunae novae iuxta elongationem Lunae a}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L024}{}{}{Sole et iuxta quantitatem latitudinis Lunae; exstante Luna in perigeo, cornua acutiora (3)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L025}{25}{}{erunt, ob disci Lunae magnitudinem, quae tunc magnitudinem disci solaris superat. Haec, Deo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L026}{}{}{volente, explicanda erant.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0179-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L027}{}{}{\Indent Si locum sphaerae caelestis in quo Luna, iuxta suam altitudinem super horizontem occi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L028}{}{}{dentalem, in initiis mensium apparet scire cupis, atque azimuth loci Lunae in circulo altitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L029}{}{}{dinis, — qui est circulus per zenith, per Lunam et per horizontem (4) transiens, — cognoscere}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L030}{30}{}{per notam, cuius directionem sequens linea visus ad locum novae Lunae pervenit, haec sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L031}{}{}{facienda. Gradui cum Luna culminanti quatuor minuta adde, ut habeas gradum cum ea tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L032}{}{}{pore apparitionis culminantem; nam radii Solis, post occasum, visionem Lunae impediunt usque}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L033}{}{p. 137.}{quo octantem circiter horae Sol sub horizonte descendat (5). Postea altitudinem apparentem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L034}{}{}{Lunae octante horae aequinoctialis post occasum Solis cognosce; et azimuth Lunae eo tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L035}{35}{}{pore deprehende iis modis quos docuimus. Deinde locum patentis et liberi horizontis deligens,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L036}{}{}{columnam vel aliquid columnae simile erige, cuius altitudo super horizontem non excedat alti-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L037}{}{}{tudinem humani corporis, ut observator possit ab ea aspicere Lunam; sit eius superficies plana,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L038}{}{}{perpendiculo aequata, et plano horizontis parallela. Circa centrum in ea sumptum describe}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L039}{}{}{circumferentiam cuiusvis diametri; directiones orientis, occidentis, meridiei et septentrionum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-body-L040}{40}{}{nota, ut in capite de linea meridiana invenienda docuimus [cap. XII], et quadrantem circuli}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0179-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Cod. et Plato A; in textu, p. 136, l. 5, corr. \ArabicRun{د}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0179-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) In figura codicis arcus quadrantis BC in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-notes_left-L003}{}{}{gradus dividitur, qua re sub schemate legitur: « ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-notes_left-L004}{}{}{« similitudinem huius quadrantis tota circumferentia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-notes_left-L005}{}{}{« dividenda est ».}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0179-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato: « erunt summitates minus acutae ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0179-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cfr. adn. 1, pag. 77.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0179-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (5) Motus medius Lunae una hora aequinoctiali}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-notes_right-L004}{}{}{est 0° 32′ 56″ (vide tab. fol. 188,v.); in octante igitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0179-notes_right-L005}{}{}{horae 0° 4′ 7″.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0180}{91}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L001}{}{}{a parte Lunae novae in 90 gradus divide. Quo facto, regulam rectam vel cannam perforatam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L002}{}{}{sume, et latus regulae vel medium locum crassitudinis cannae super centrum circuli pone, ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L003}{}{}{directionem novae Lunae respectu puncti orientis vel occidentis, prout nova Luna ab alterutra}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L004}{}{}{parte sit; deinde astrolabium tuae manui suspendens, extremam alhidadam ut altitudinem ap-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L005}{5}{}{parentem Lunae quam invenisti pone; et regulae vel cannae extremitatem, quae versus Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L006}{}{}{nam novam est, a superficie circuli, aliquid ei supponendo, eleva, ita tamen ut a directione}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L007}{}{}{Lunae et a centro circuli nullo modo deflectat, sed extremitatum altera in Lunam versus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L008}{}{}{assurgat, altera ad oculos deprimatur; qua re visus per utrumque alhidadae foramen et per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L009}{}{}{latus regulae vel interiorem cannae partem transeat, atque a loco oculorum ad locum Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L010}{10}{}{lineā rectā protendatur. Cum observator igitur tempore apparitionis inspexerit, videbit Lunam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L011}{}{}{novam ad directionem lateris regulae vel cannae. Haec, si Deus vult, est figura quam descri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L012}{}{}{psimus (\textbf{\textit{f}}).}
\par\vspace{-\ht\strutbox}\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{0.611\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0180-body-F01}{}\input{figures/AB01-PDF0180-F01}\end{minipage}\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{0.369\FullSourceWidth}\vspace{0pt}\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0.631\FullSourceWidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0180-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]:}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L014}{}{}{Circa centrum E cir-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L015}{15}{}{cumferentiam ABCD}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L016}{}{}{describamus; sit E}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L017}{}{}{centrum circuli hori-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L018}{}{}{zontis in superficie}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L019}{}{}{plana, scilicet [pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L020}{20}{}{iectio] zenith; sit A}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L021}{}{}{punctum meridiei, B}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L022}{}{}{orientis, C septentrio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L023}{}{}{num, D occidentis.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L024}{}{}{Rectas AC, BD du-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L025}{25}{}{camus, ponentes Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L026}{}{p. 138.}{nam a parte occiden-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L027}{}{}{tali, idest in quadran-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L028}{}{}{te AD. Sit punctum}
\end{minipage}\par
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L029}{}{}{B in ecliptica initium Arietis; erit igitur D initium Librae, et ita habebimus ascendens et oc-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L030}{30}{}{cidens eclipticae. Sit arcus DLB hemisphaerium australe eclipticae; patet lineam CL esse lo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L031}{}{}{cum initii Capricorni, quod in linea medii Caeli reperitur (1). Gradus cum Luna culminans sit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L032}{}{}{punctum H eclipticae, \textit{scilicet initium Scorpionis} (2); locus Lunae iuxta eius latitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L033}{}{}{australem, F. Ponamus denique lineam KEFG (3) locum lateris regulae vel interioris partis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L034}{}{}{cannae, quae per centrum circuli, et locum Lunae \textit{et gradum cum Luna culminantem}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L035}{35}{}{transit. Nunc arcum DG horizontis inveniamus. Manifeste est \textit{arcus HG altitudo gradus}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L036}{}{}{\textit{cum quo Luna culminat, super horizontem; et} arcus FG altitudo Lunae. Item arcus AL}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L037}{}{}{est altitudo initii Capricorni in medio Caelo; \textit{arcus DH arcum eclipticae inter initium Li-}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L038}{}{}{\textit{brae et gradum cum Luna culminantem;} punctum G directio Lunae. Itaque arcus DG}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L039}{}{}{horizontis est elongatio directionis Lunae a puncto occidentis (4) aequinoctialis; et si linea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L040}{40}{}{KG a punctis E et G in aerem elevetur quantitate altitudinis Lunae in astrolabio signata,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-body-L041}{}{}{locus K versus Terram depressus fiet; visus autem per ambo alhidadae foramina, quae sunt}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0180-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) L est Capricorni initium.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0180-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Quae Italicis litteris sunt impressa, quam-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-notes_left-L003}{}{}{quam in codice et Platone exstant, procul dubio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-notes_left-L004}{}{}{deleri debent; cfr. adn. \textbf{\textit{f}}.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0180-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod. et Plato KEHFG.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0180-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0180-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (4) I. e. azimuth Lunae.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0181}{92}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L001}{}{}{M et K, transibit; MG fiet una linea recta; et observator a loco M vel K Lunam novam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L002}{}{}{per illam notam in directione lineae KG procul dubio videbit, si aer clarus et purus fuerit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L003}{}{}{Aer autem mutabilis turbidusque visionem Lunae in ea regione impediet, et Luna fortasse in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L004}{}{}{aliis locis eiusdem latitudinis apparebit, cum necesse non sit ubicumque aerem turbidum esse.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L005}{5}{}{Qua re etiam fieri potest ut Luna nova, in urbe invisibilis, in pagis prope urbem conspici}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L006}{}{}{possit.}
\par\vspace{4.28mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0181-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L007}{}{}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XLII.}}
\par\vspace{3.00mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0181-H08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L008}{10}{}{\CenterLine{De syzygiarum calculo quolibet Terrae loco ad aeram Romanorum}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0181-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{et aeram Coptorum.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{4.18mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0181-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L010}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Si coniunctiones et oppositiones quolibet mense Romano supputare vis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L011}{}{}{annos a Dhū ’l–qarnayn sume, annum in quo es non numerans nisi subāṭ [i. e. Februarius]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L012}{15}{p. 139.}{praeterierit (1); numerum inventum in columna annorum collectorum [ab aera], in tabulis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L013}{}{}{coniunctionum aut oppositionum (2), prout alterutram vis, quaere; et si numerus tuo aequalis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L014}{}{}{ibi non reperiatur, numerum statim in tabula inferiorem sume. Postea quod respondet in qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L015}{}{}{tuor columnis dierum, motus medii Solis Lunaeque, anomaliae Lunae, et motus latitudinis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L016}{}{}{nota. Quo facto, si tibi remanent anni, praeter eos quos in tabula reperisti, eos in columna}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L017}{20}{}{annorum singulorum quaere; et quod in quatuor reliquis columnis respondet, ordinatim sub}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L018}{}{}{numeris similibus antea inventis scribe. Deinde e diebus mensis lunaris, qui in prima quatuor}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L019}{}{}{columnarum [in tabula mensium] descripti sunt, id accipe quod respondet mensi integro prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L020}{}{}{cedenti mensem in quo calculus ducitur; idque adde diebus qui ex annis collectis et singulis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L021}{}{}{iam provenerant. Si summa excedit dies mensium Romanorum sub eo integro mense descri-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L022}{25}{}{ptos, sed minor est quam dies indicati sub mense in quo calculus ducitur, sume dies mensi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L023}{}{}{integro [mensem tuum praecedenti] respondentes, et quod sub iis in tribus reliquis columnis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L024}{}{}{invenitur nota. Si autem dierum summa excedit dies Romanos sub mense in quo calculum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L025}{}{}{ducis notatos, sume dies mensis qui mensem integrum praecedit, et quod in tribus residuis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L026}{}{}{columnis respondet, ordinatim scribe sub numeris qui e tabulis annorum singulorum et colle-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L027}{30}{}{ctorum provenerant. Numeros repertos in quatuor summas, pro quatuor columnis, collige.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L028}{}{}{Postea dies Romanos respondentes mensi integro qui mensem calculi praecedit, deme de summa}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L029}{}{}{dierum provenientium e tribus tabulis annorum collectorum, annorum singulorum, et men-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L030}{}{}{sium; dies et minuta residua sunt dies elapsi e mense in quo calculum ducis, et horae aequi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L031}{}{}{noctiales post meridiem diei praeteriti usque ad tempus coniunctionis aut oppositionis, iuxta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L032}{35}{}{motus medios Solis et Lunae. [Ex tribus reliquis columnis autem proveniunt locus Solis et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L033}{}{}{Lunae, iuxta ipsorum motus medios] (3), anomalia Lunae, et motus in latitudine. [Lunae mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L034}{}{}{tus medius] idem est ac motus medius Solis tempore [coniunctionis; tempore autem] opposi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L035}{}{}{tionis necessarie Lunae motus medius erit motui [i. e. loco] medio Solis oppositus.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0181-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent Si coniunctiones et oppositiones vis ad aeram Coptorum (4) supputare, annos a Dhū ’l–qar-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0181-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Ad incommodum vitandum quod distinctio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_left-L002}{}{}{annorum communium et bisextilium calculis infert,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_left-L003}{}{}{epochae motuum Solis, Lunae et planetarum ad an-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_left-L004}{}{}{num fictum referuntur, qui die I ādhār (Martii) prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_left-L005}{}{}{cedentis mensem aylūl (Septembrem) anni dati in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_left-L006}{}{}{cipit. Vide adn. \textbf{\textit{a}} ad cap. XXXIII.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0181-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) Fol. 192,v. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0181-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (3) Addidi, Platonem sequens. Quae statim in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_right-L003}{}{}{fra supplevi, Plato quoque non habet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0181-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (4) Est aera Augusti vel Alexandrina, quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_right-L005}{}{}{al-Battānī incipit a 29 Aug. anni 25 ante Chr. n.;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0181-notes_right-L006}{}{}{cfr. adn. \textbf{\textit{b}} ad cap. XXXII, et pag. 69 (adn. 3).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0182}{93}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L001}{}{}{nayn sume cum anno in quo es, licet unus dies tantum mensis aylūl [i. e. Septembris] prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L002}{}{p. 140.}{terierit; de annis detrahe 287 (1), et residui quartam partem accipe, quam dies quartae partis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L003}{}{}{vocamus. Fractiones neglige; si autem nulla fractio exstet, annus bisextilis erit, qua re de}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L004}{}{}{diebus quartae partis unum diem demes, si subāṭ, tunc 29 (2) dies complectens, nondum prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L005}{5}{}{terierit, at diem demptum restitues diebus quartae partis si subāṭ totus iam elapsus erit. Die-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L006}{}{}{bus quartae partis semper tres dies adiice, quibus Copti in initio mensis thōth praecedunt Grae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L007}{}{}{cos; quo facto, diebus ita repertis adde dies ab initio aylūl ad finem mensis Romani qui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L008}{}{}{mensem tantum inceptum praecedit. Si summa excedit 365 dies, ab ea 365 deme, et annis post}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L009}{}{}{Dhū ’l–qarnayn annum adde; at si annus bisextilis est, atque iam subāṭ praeteriit, 366 dies}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L010}{10}{}{pro eo anno computandi sunt. Dies praeter annos integros nunc residui, seorsum scribantur.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L011}{}{}{Annos a Dhū ’l–qarnayn, eo anno, si necesse erat, auctos, in columna annorum Aegyptiorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L012}{}{}{qui [ab aera] collecti sunt et cum intervallo 25 (3) descripti, in tabula coniunctionis aut oppo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L013}{}{}{sitionis, prout alterutram vis, quaere; si numerum tuo aequalem non invenis, numerum sta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L014}{}{}{tim inferiorem sume. Quod ei in quatuor columnis respondet, ut supra diximus nota. Annos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L015}{15}{}{residuos in columna annorum singulorum quaere et quod iis respondet in quatuor columnis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L016}{}{}{similiter sume. Postea quoties potes 30 de diebus deme; numerus vicium indicat menses inte-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L017}{}{}{gros, quos in tabula mensium Coptorum quaeres; quod in dierum columna respondet, adde}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L018}{}{}{diebus quos in tabulis annorum collectorum et singulorum reperisti; deinde, si summa aequat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L019}{}{}{numerum dierum Copticorum, aut eum superat minus quam spatio mensis lunaris, eos dies}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L020}{20}{}{serva, nec non quod sub iis in tribus reliquis columnis indicatur. At si summa excedit dies}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L021}{}{}{Copticos magis quam mense lunari, de numero mensium Coptorum integrorum, quos in tabula}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L022}{}{}{quaesieras, unum mensem deme; quod residuo respondet in quatuor columnis, scribe, et, ut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L023}{}{}{supra diximus, summas confice. De diebus ita collectis deme dies Copticos; dies et minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L024}{}{}{residua erunt dies et horae coniunctionis et oppositionis praeteriti ex mense in quo calculum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L025}{25}{}{duxisti. E tribus reliquis columnis autem provenient motus medius Solis Lunaeque, anomalia}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L026}{}{}{Lunae, et motus latitudinis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0182-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L027}{}{p. 141.}{\Indent His omnibus quavis aera cognitis, minuta cum diebus collecta observa; singula 2 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L028}{}{}{nuta ut unam horam aequinoctialem computa; quod 2 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ minuta non perficit, indicat partes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L029}{}{}{horae. Dies et horae hoc modo provenientes, sunt dies coniunctionis aut oppositionis mediae,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L030}{30}{}{atque horae pomeridianae in urbe ar–Raqqah. Idque serva. — Postea medium motum Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L031}{}{}{[et Lunae] alio loco scribe, alterum locum Soli, alterum Lunae tribuens; Solem et Lunam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L032}{}{}{ut consuemus corrige, sed in Luna tantum aequatione simplici utere, nam Luna a Sole tantum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L033}{}{}{non distat ut e neglecta secunda aequatione error sensibilis proveniat. Si eodem minuto aequales}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L034}{}{}{Sol et Luna reperiuntur, hoc est tempus verae coniunctionis aut oppositionis; igitur propter}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L035}{35}{}{motum latitudinis fac ut pro medio motu Lunae, scilicet aequationem simplicem adde motui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L036}{}{}{latitudinis si eam iam motui medio Lunae addideris, aut de eo deme, si eam de medio motu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L037}{}{}{Lunae dempseris.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0182-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L038}{}{}{\Indent Si autem locus Solis a loco Lunae differt, $\qfrac{1}{6}$ + $\qfrac{1}{8}$ [= $\qfrac{7}{24}$] differentiae (4) adde anoma-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L039}{}{}{liae lunari, si gradus loci Solis superant gradus loci Lunae; sed de anomalia deme, si locus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L040}{40}{}{Solis minor est loco Lunae. Proveniet anomalia aequata, quam in columna numerorum in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-body-L041}{}{}{tabulis aequationis Lunae (5) quaere, et aequationem simplicem ei respondentem in secunda}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0182-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Plato perperam 587; cfr. idem cap. XXXII,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-notes_left-L002}{}{}{pag. 69 (adn. 3) et 70.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0182-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Plato 59.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0182-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato 50. — Tabulae fol. 170,v.-172,r.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0182-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cur ita operandum sit auctor p. 95 explicat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0182-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (5) Fol. 189,v. et seqq. — Duplex numerorum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-notes_right-L003}{}{}{anomaliae series in numero columnarum non com-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0182-notes_right-L004}{}{}{putatur. Cfr. p. 80, adn. 4.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0183}{94}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L001}{}{}{columna sume. Si anomalia aequata est minor 180 gradibus, aequationem de motu medio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L002}{}{}{Lunae et de motu latitudinis deme; si anomalia 180° superat, aequationem adde motui medio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L003}{}{}{Lunae et motui latitudinis. Motus ita repertus est locus verus Lunae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Postea spatium inter Solem et Lunam cognosce; denique motum horarium Solis et Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L005}{5}{}{nae hoc modo inveni: Anomaliam aequatam Lunae, qua usus es ad aequationem lunarem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L006}{}{}{sumendam, item anomaliam Solis, per quam aequationem solarem deprehendisti, quaere in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L007}{}{}{tabula motuum inaequalium Solis et Lunae (1), in columna numerorum qui cum intervallo}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L008}{}{}{sex unitatum sunt descripti; quod sub iis in columna motus Solis et in columna motus Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L009}{}{}{respondet sume, interpolatione utens, post additas motui Lunae aut ab eo detractas secundas,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L010}{10}{}{quas sub differentia inter Solem et Lunam eo modo inveneris ut in illo capite prope illas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L011}{}{}{tabulas (2) docuimus (\textbf{\textit{a}}). Postea motum Solis de motu Lunae deme; remanet praecessio inae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L012}{}{}{qualis horaria Lunae respectu Solis (3). Spatium inter Solem et Lunam divide per hanc prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L013}{}{}{cessionem lunarem; exibunt horae differentiae. Si differentia ob maiorem Solis longitudinem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L014}{}{p. 142.}{evenit, horas differentiae adde horis mediae coniunctionis in tabula repertis; si autem differen-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L015}{15}{}{tia ob maiorem Lunae locum accidit, eas de horis mediae coniunctionis deme. Exibunt horae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L016}{}{}{adproximatae coniunctionis verae; de quibus, si 24 (4) excedunt, 24 deme, et diem adde diebus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L017}{}{}{praeteritis. — Cum ab horis mediae coniunctionis debeas horas differentiae demere, hae autem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L018}{}{}{maiores sint horis coniunctionis, de diebus mensis praeteritis diem deme, eumque 24 horas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L019}{}{}{computa quas horis coniunctionis addes; a summa detrahe horas differentiae, et exibunt horae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L020}{20}{}{aequinoctiales in urbe ar–Raqqah transactae post meridiem diei qui tibi ex diebus mensis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L021}{}{}{elapsis provenerat. — Horas differentiae multiplica in motum horarium Solis et in motum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L022}{}{}{horarium Lunae. Si differentia ob maiorem Solis longitudinem orta erat, productum motus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L023}{}{}{Solis adde loco Solis, productum motus Lunae loco Lunae et motui latitudinis; item iis adiice}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L024}{}{}{motum nodi borealis in illarum horarum spatio. Si vero differentia ob maiorem Lunae lon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L025}{25}{}{gitudinem exstiterat, pro additione subductione utere, ut Solis et Lunae locum veraciter scias.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L026}{}{}{\Indent Si alio modo, veritati propinquo, facere mavis, duodecimam partem differentiae [longitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L027}{}{}{dinalis] inter Solem et Lunam cognosce (5). Si ob maiorem Solis longitudinem differentia orta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L028}{}{}{est, duodecimam partem illam Soli adde; et differentiam totam, cum duodecima parte, adiice}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L029}{}{}{Lunae et motui latitudinis. Exstante contra differentia ob maiorem longitudinem Lunae, duo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L030}{30}{}{decimam partem de Sole, et differentiam totam cum duodecima parte de Luna [atque de motu}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L031}{}{}{latitudinis] (6) deme. Sol et Luna in eodem minuto aequales fient. Postea [differentiam to-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L032}{}{}{tam] (7) et duodecimam partem per motum horarium inaequalem Lunae divide; exibunt horae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L033}{}{}{differentiae, quas de horis mediae coniunctionis demes, aut iis addes, prout ob maiorem Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L034}{}{}{aut Solis longitudinem differentia exstiterit (8). — Sed prior methodus verior est.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L035}{35}{}{\Indent Anomalia quoque Lunae tempore [coniunctionis et oppositionis] est cognoscenda. Horas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L036}{}{}{differentiae quaere in tabula horarum, ibique iter anomaliae Lunae sume, quod anomaliae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L037}{}{}{lunari aequatae addes vel ab ea demes, prout ex maiore Solis aut Lunae longitudine diffe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L038}{}{}{rentia provenerit; exibit anomalia aequata Lunae tempore coniunctionis, si coniunctionem sup-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-body-L039}{}{}{putas, aut anomalia lunaris aequata (9) tempore oppositionis, si oppositionem consideras. Postea}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Fol. 194,v.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Vide caput H (in fine capitum) atque tabu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_left-L003}{}{}{lam fol. 194,v.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) I. e. motus synodicus vel relativus Lunae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (4) Plato hic et infra: 54.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (5) Haec duodecima pars e motu Solis oritur.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (6) Addidi, Platonem sequens.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (7) Addidi, Plat. sequens.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (8) Est methodus descripta in \textit{Almag.} VI, 4 (ed.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_right-L004}{}{}{Halma, t. I, p. 386).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0183-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (9) Cod. et Plato addunt: « et motus latitudi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0183-notes_right-L006}{}{}{« nis ».}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0184}{95}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L001}{}{p. 143.}{loco Lunae, quem fecisti ut motum medium (1) Solis, adde 180°; habebis locum verum Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L002}{}{}{nae, in quo ipsa, a parte loco Solis contraria, apparet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0184-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Cum horas aequinoctiales adproximatas coniunctionis, quae mediae sunt, cognoveris, eas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L004}{}{}{in horas dierum inaequalium hoc modo converte: Gradum Solis in tabulis sphaerae rectae (2)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L005}{5}{}{quaere; gradus minutaque in columna aequationis nychthemerōn respondentia divide per 15;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L006}{}{}{quotiens horas indicat, residuum fractiones horae. Id semper horis mediis verae coniunctio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L007}{}{}{nis (3) adde; habebis horas aequinoctiales verae coniunctionis, conversas ad nychthemera inae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L008}{}{}{qualia, post meridiem in urbe ar–Raqqah. Eas denique poteris in horas cuiusvis loci conver-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L009}{}{}{tere: idest differentiam longitudinis inter urbem tuam et ar–Raqqah, cuius longitudo 73° 15′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L010}{10}{}{complectitur, per 15 divide; exibunt horae vel partes horae, quas horis veris coniunctionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L011}{}{}{iam repertis addes, aut ab iis demes, prout longitudo tui loci maior aut minor longitudine ar–}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L012}{}{}{–Raqqah fuerit. Exibunt horae aequinoctiales verae post meridiem tui loci.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0184-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L013}{}{}{\Indent Si ascendens [tempore] coniunctionis scire vis, has horas omnes in 15° multiplica; pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L014}{}{}{ductum adde ascensionibus rectis gradus Solis, et hac summa, ut alibi docuimus [cap. XVI}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L015}{15}{}{et XXXV], ascendens et medium Caelum invenies.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0184-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L016}{}{}{\Indent Si horas aequinoctiales memoratas in temporales convertere vis, eas in 15° multiplica, et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L017}{}{}{productum serva. Postea horas noctis et diei in eo climate per gradum Solis deprehende. De}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L018}{}{}{producto servato deme tempora horaria diei usque ad sex horarum finem; si minus sex horis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L019}{}{}{[productum illud] est, ei sex horas temporales quae sunt ab ortu Solis ad meridiem, adde.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L020}{20}{}{Exibunt horae temporariae diei ab ortu Solis ad tempus coniunctionis. Si, post sex horas inte-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L021}{}{}{gras detractas, residuum habes, ab eo tempora horaria noctis, usque ad finem 12 (4) hora-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L022}{}{}{rum detrahe; et si iterum aliquid superest, tempora horaria diei denuo deme; quod exibit}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L023}{}{}{erunt horae elapsae post crastinum ortum Solis. His horis ascendens, ut docuimus, inveni.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L024}{}{}{Manifeste si horae coniunctionis superant dimidiam partem horarum diurnarum aequinoctialium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L025}{25}{p. 144.}{illius nychthemeri, atque eas de 12 horis demis si minores quam 12 exstent, remanent horae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L026}{}{}{aequinoctiales quibus coniunctio praecedit tempus mediae noctis. Si contra horae superent 12}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L027}{}{}{usque ad finem dimidiae partis horarum aequinoctialium illius noctis, et ab iis 12 demas, rema-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L028}{}{}{nebunt horae aequinoctiales quibus coniunctio tempus mediae noctis sequitur. Excedentibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L029}{}{}{denique 12 horas simul cum dimidia parte addita horarum noctis, si ab iis 24 (5) demis, exeunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L030}{30}{}{horae aequinoctiales quibus coniunctio tempus crastinae mediae noctis praecedit. — Iuxta ea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L031}{}{}{quae diximus, tempus coniunctionis et oppositionis, loca Solis et Lunae, anomaliam Lunae et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L032}{}{}{motum latitudinis iis temporibus scire potes.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0184-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L033}{}{}{\Indent Causam qua anomalia Lunae per $\qfrac{1}{6}$ + $\qfrac{1}{8}$ [= $\qfrac{7}{24}$] partem differentiae aequanda est,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L034}{}{}{nunc explicabimus. Non semper accidit, nec necessarium est, ut tempus mediae coniunctionis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L035}{35}{}{sit quoque tempus coniunctionis verae; si igitur id quod anomaliae ob duplam elongationem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L036}{}{}{Lunae a Sole contingit negligimus, tempore coniunctionis vel oppositionis differentia haberi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L037}{}{}{potest, cuius maxima quantitas quadrantem horae attingit. Nam, cum aequatio Solis 2° sit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L038}{}{}{aequatio Lunae 3°, si harum aequationum altera a medio itinere crescat, altera ab eo decrescat,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L039}{}{}{simul efficient 5°, quorum duplum 10° est dupla elongatio. Tunc invenies aequationem ano-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L040}{40}{}{maliae vel in augmento vel in decremento fere 1° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ attingere, quod circiter $\qfrac{7}{24}$ differentiae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-body-L041}{}{}{est. Exstante Luna eo epicycli loco, quo eius aequatio correcta debet 3° esse, portio perti-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0184-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) I. e. quem ut longitudinem mediam Solis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-notes_left-L002}{}{}{invenisti.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0184-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Fol. 179,v. et seqq.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0184-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod.: « horis verae mediae coniunctionis ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0184-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (4) Plato 15, ut semper quoque infra.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0184-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0184-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (5) Plato 54.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0185}{96}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L001}{}{}{nens ad illum 1° $\qfrac{1}{2}$ erit circiter $\qfrac{1}{8}$ gradus, ob praecessionem (1) Lunae [tunc] fere quartam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L002}{}{}{partem horae attingentem (\textbf{\textit{b}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent In hoc Ptolemaeus (2) maximas aequationes consideravit, ita ut Lunae 5° et Soli 2° 23′}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L004}{}{}{tribuerit, iuxta mensuram qua usus est; tunc igitur differentia inter Solem et Lunam 7° 23′ (3)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L005}{5}{}{esset, eiusque duplum 14° 46′ fere. Res cum ita sint, non potest [tamen] aliquid amplius quam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L006}{}{}{$\qfrac{1}{8}$ horae inveniri ut memoravimus; sed cum aequatio Lunae 5° est, portio unius gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L007}{}{}{vel duorum quae anomaliae additur vel ab ea demitur, fit quantitas permodica et insensibilis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L008}{}{}{quae maior est cum [anomalia correcta Lunae] 3° complectatur, quam cum [eadem anomalia]}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L009}{}{}{sit horum 5 graduum (4). In hoc ergo res nonnisi ut diximus esse potest (\textbf{\textit{c}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L010}{10}{p. 145.}{\Indent Manifeste si differentiam [longitudinis] inter Solem et Lunam diviserimus per praecessio-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L011}{}{}{nem Lunae, quae ex anomalia inter mediam et veram coniunctionem deprehenditur, id certius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L012}{}{}{et verius cognoscemus. Haec anomalia autem ita reperitur: semidifferentiae inter Solem et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L013}{}{}{Lunam adde ipsius duodecimam partem (5); summam ab anomalia aequata deme, vel anomaliae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L014}{}{}{adde, prout differentia ob maiorem Lunae vel Solis longitudinem contingit; veraciter ita co-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L015}{15}{}{gnosces anomaliam Lunae iuxta eius motum medium inter veram et mediam coniunctionem.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L016}{}{}{Per eam deprehendes motum horarium Lunae; de quo motum horarium Solis deme; et prae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L017}{}{}{cessione Lunae tunc residua utere in divisione differentiae [longitudinis].}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L018}{}{}{\Indent Si vis computare tempora methodo qua nychthemerum 60 minutorum esse ponitur, horas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L019}{}{}{aequinoctiales coniunctionis, quae in tua urbe post meridiem sint, multiplica in 2 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ minuta;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L020}{20}{}{si productum est 30 minutorum, coniunctio media nocte evenit; si minus quam 30, ante me-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L021}{}{}{diam noctem; si maius, post mediam noctem. Minuta inventa gradus considera, pro minutis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L022}{}{}{gradus (6), pro secundis minuta ponens; deinde tempora horaria diei et noctis inveni. Si gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L023}{}{}{illi minores sunt temporibus horariis diurnis, coniunctio est diurna; ipsi autem per sextam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L024}{}{}{partem temporum horariorum diei divisi, dant horas temporales pomeridianas. Si contra gradus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L025}{25}{}{superant tempora horaria diei usque ad finem 30 graduum, de iis tempora horaria diurna deme,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L026}{}{}{et residuum per sextam partem temporum horarum noctis divide; exibunt horae temporales}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L027}{}{}{post initium noctis versus mediam noctem elapsae. Gradibus denique 30 superantibus, ab iis 30}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L028}{}{}{deme; residuum, si minus est temporibus horariis noctis, divide per sextam partem temporum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L029}{}{}{horarum noctis, ut horas temporales post mediam noctem habeas. Et si gradus tempora ho-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L030}{30}{}{raria noctis superant, de iis tempora horaria nocturna deme; residuum divide per tempora}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L031}{}{}{horaria diei; exibunt, Deo volente, horae temporales post ortum Solis crastini diei elapsae.}
\par\vspace{3.88mm}
\par\fontsize{14.5}{17}\selectfont
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-H32}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L032}{}{p. 146.}{\CenterLine{CAPUT XLIII.}}
\par\vspace{2.94mm}
\par\fontsize{14.2}{16}\selectfont
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L033}{35}{}{De cognitione eclipsium Lunae calculis et tabulis; de earum quantita-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L034}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}tibus et temporibus; denique de plaga in qua obscuratio et emersio}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L035}{}{}{\mbox{}\rlap{\hspace*{7.55mm}fiunt in circulis horizontum qui pro diversis Terrae locis variant.}}
\par\fontsize{11.1}{13.8}\selectfont
\par\vspace{3.58mm}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-P04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent Dicit [auctor]: Cum eclipses Lunae scire vis, motum medium latitudinis in oppositionibus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-body-L037}{40}{}{observa, nam si inter terminos eclipsium, in pagina mensium syzygiarum descriptos (7), est,}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) I. e. motum synodicum.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) \textit{Almag.} VI, 5 (Halma, t. I, p. 394); ponit ibi}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_left-L003}{}{}{5° 1′, postea ergo \SunSym{} — \MoonSym{} = 7° 24′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) Plato 7° 53′.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (4) Textus, ut Plato, satis obscure: « ut ea ad}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_left-L006}{}{}{« 3° maioris est differentiae quam ad eos 5° ».}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (5) Duodecima fere pars elongationis oritur e}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_right-L002}{}{}{motu Solis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (6) Cod. hic et infra: « signa (zodiaci) ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0185-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (7) Cod. ponit terminos eclipticos in pagina}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_right-L005}{}{}{annorum singulorum Aegyptiorum (fol. 172,r.), et in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0185-notes_right-L006}{}{}{pagina motuum horariorum (fol. 194,v.).}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0186}{97}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L001}{}{}{Luna deficere potest; sed si terminis illis maior aut minor reperitur, Luna deficere nequit.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L002}{}{}{Si eclipsis fieri potest, observa motum latitudinis aequatum ad tempus oppositionis; si 360°}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L003}{}{}{complectitur, Luna est in nodo Capitis; si 180°, Luna est in nodo Caudae; si alterutro numero}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L004}{}{}{maior aut minor reperitur, pro ratione augmenti aut deminutionis Luna est nodo vicina aut}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L005}{5}{}{remota. Exstante Luna in nodis, eclipsis maxima fit; si ab alterutro nodo ante vel retro magis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L006}{}{}{quam 12 gradibus distat, Luna non deficit; si a nodo distat sed minus quam 12 gradibus,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L007}{}{}{deficit iuxta quantitatem suae distantiae a nodo. Si oppositio noctu aut prope ortum vel occa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L008}{}{}{sum Solis evenit, eclipsis tota aut eius pars tantum apparet, iuxta temporis quantitatem.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L009}{}{}{\Indent Cum Lunam defecturam et eclipsim omnino aut partim apparituram esse scias, motum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L010}{10}{}{latitudinis correctum ad tempus coniunctionis quaere in tabula aequationis (1), ut Lunae lati-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L011}{}{}{tudinem accipias atque eius plagam in Caelo deprehendas. Aut, si mavis, latitudinem inveni per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L012}{}{}{distantiam Lunae a nodo. Exibit Lunae latitudo [vera ad medium eclipsis] (2). Anomaliam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L013}{}{}{Lunae tempore oppositionis quaere in tabulis correctionis (3); minuta quae ei in tertia columna,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L014}{}{}{portiones longinquitatis indicante, respondent vide quota pars ex 60 sint, et partem propor-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L015}{15}{}{tionalem sume ex iis 5 $\qfrac{3}{4}$ (4) minutis quibus diametrus Lunae variat; eam partem semper}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L016}{}{p. 147.}{adde 0° 29′ 30″ (5) quae sunt diametrus Lunae in maxima distantia; remanet diametrus ae-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L017}{}{}{quata Lunae. Similiter ex 7 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ minutis quibus semidiametrus umbrae variat, sume partem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L018}{}{}{proportionalem ad minuta tertiae columnae; eam adde 38 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ minutis quae continentur semi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L019}{}{}{diametro umbrae in maxima distantia [geocentrica] Lunae; exibit semidiametrus (6) aequata}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L020}{20}{}{umbrae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Si eadem calculo aliter scire vis, motum horarium inaequalem Lunae multiplica in 6}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L022}{}{}{minus $\qfrac{1}{8}$ [i. e. in 5 $\qfrac{7}{8}$] (7); exibunt minuta quorum sexta pars erit diametrus aequata Lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L023}{}{}{nae. Si semidiametrum aequatam umbrae scire vis, dimidiam diametrum [aequatam] Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L024}{}{}{multiplica in 2 $\qfrac{3}{5}$ (8); productum erit semidiametrus aequata umbrae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L025}{25}{}{\Indent Diametro Lunae et semidiametro umbrae alterutro modo cognitis, semidiametrum aequa-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L026}{}{}{tam Lunae adde semidiametro aequatae umbrae; summa est dimidia pars duarum diametro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L027}{}{}{rum. Si haec aequalis est latitudini verae Lunae, Luna extremam umbrae lineam tantum tan-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L028}{}{}{get et nullo modo deficiet. Si latitudo minor est semisumma diametrorum, latitudinem de hac}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L029}{}{}{dimidia summa deme; si residuum diametrum lunarem aequat, Luna deficiet tota, sed [in um-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L030}{30}{}{bra] non morabitur; si contra residuum superat diametrum, Luna deficiet tota cum mora}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L031}{}{}{[in umbra]. Si denique residuum diametro lunari minus reperitur, Luna non deficiet tota;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L032}{}{}{minuta igitur residui in 12 (9) multiplica et productum per diametrum aequatam Lunae di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L033}{}{}{vide, ut cognoscas quod diametri lunaris deficit, iuxta rationem qua diametrus Lunae 12 partes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L034}{}{}{complectitur, digitos eclipsis appellatas. Si Luna moram habet, diametrum lunarem aequatam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-body-L035}{35}{}{ab iis minutis residuis deme; remanent minuta morae (\textbf{\textit{a}}). Haec etiam in 12 multiplica; pro-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Fol. 189,v. et seqq.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Addidi, Platonem sequens.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Fol. 195,r., ubi duae series numerorum ar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L004}{}{}{gumenti pro duabus columnis supputantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (4) Ita cod. et Plato (5 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$); sed deberent}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L006}{}{}{esse 5 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$ + $\qfrac{1}{12}$ (= 5 $\qfrac{5}{6}$). Cfr. cap. XXX, ubi dia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L007}{}{}{metrus lunaris in apogeo statuitur 29′ 30″, in pe-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L008}{}{}{rigeo 35′ 20″.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L009}{}{}{\Indent (5) Secundae in cod. 40″.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L010}{}{}{\Indent (6) Cod. « diametrus ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_left-L011}{}{}{\Indent (7) Sunt circiter variatio diametri lunaris ab}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L001}{}{}{apogeo ad perigeum; cfr. quae parum supra in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L002}{}{}{adn. 4 huius paginae, dixi. Rectius pag. 61, l. 2, au-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L003}{}{}{ctor 5′ 50″ (i. e. 5′ $\qfrac{5}{6}$) scripserat.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (8) Est media ratio diametri lunaris ad diame-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L005}{}{}{trum umbrae; e cap. XXX, p. 58 et 60-61, elicitur,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L006}{}{}{in minima distantia Lunae,}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-H07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L007}{}{}{\CenterLine{diam. Lunae : diam. umbrae = 35 $\qfrac{1}{3}$ : 92 = 2 $\qfrac{3}{5}$;}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L008}{}{}{in maxima distantia autem}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-H09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L009}{}{}{\CenterLine{diam. Lunae : diam. umbrae = 29 $\qfrac{1}{2}$ : 77 = 2 $\qfrac{36}{58}$.}}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0186-N09}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L010}{}{}{\Indent (9) Plato hic et infra semper 15, quod recte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0186-notes_right-L011}{}{}{inusitatum censebat \Name{Delambre}, p. 49.}
\end{minipage}
\par\vspace{5mm}\hfill{\fontsize{8}{10}\selectfont 13}\hspace{10mm}
\DSource{0187}{98}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L001}{}{}{ductum per diametrum Lunae divide; digitos quotientis adde duodecim digitis qui totam dia-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L002}{}{}{metrum Lunae perficiunt; habebis digitos eclipsis ab initio ad medium eclipsis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0187-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L003}{}{}{\Indent Aut, si mavis, minuta illa, e dimidia diametrorum parte residua, sive maiora sunt sive}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L004}{}{}{minora diametro Lunae, multiplica in 12; productum per diametrum Lunae divide, et exibunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L005}{5}{}{digiti eclipsis. Postea de quadrato dimidiae summae duarum diametrorum deme quadratum lati-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L006}{}{}{tudinis Lunae; radix residui indicat minuta immersionis et morae simul, si Luna moram facit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L007}{}{}{vel minuta immersionis tantum, si Luna non moratur. Minuta per praecessionem Lunae (1)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L008}{}{p. 148.}{divide; exibunt horae immersionis aut horae immersionis et morae simul, ut [antea cum mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L009}{}{}{nutis] evenerat; eas deme ab horis oppositionis, quae sunt horae medii eclipsis, et remanent}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L010}{10}{}{horae initii eclipsis; aut horis medii adde, et proveniunt horae aequinoctiales finis emersionis.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L011}{}{}{Si Luna moram facit, diametrum aequatam Lunae de semisumma diametrorum deme; rema-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L012}{}{}{nent minuta totius morae (2). Ab eorum quadrato deme quadratum latitudinis Lunae; radicem}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L013}{}{}{residui divide per praecessionem Lunae; habebis horas morae. Has si de horis medii eclipsis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L014}{}{}{demis, habes horas initii morae; si horis medii addis, horas initii emersionis cognoscis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0187-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L015}{15}{}{\Indent Cum Luna non deficit tota, aut tota deficit sed absque mora, tria tempora in eclipsi ha-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L016}{}{}{bentur; deficiente vero Luna tota cum mora [in umbra], eclipsis quinque tempora habet; quae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L017}{}{}{tamen modo tantum generali designantur, sed calculo non possunt certissime definiri. Nam}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L018}{}{}{latitudo Lunae ab initio ad medium eclipsis, et a medio ad finem emersionis variat; variant}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L019}{}{}{igitur etiam quantitates temporum quae ab utraque parte medii sunt; medium solum non va-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L020}{20}{}{riat. Si id ita accurate scire vis ut ob eius causam errores in calculo non fiant, minutis im-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L021}{}{}{mersionis et morae, aut minutis immersionis, prout evenit, — et haec sunt eadem minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L022}{}{}{quae per praecessionem Lunae dividere te supra iussi, — adde duodecimam ipsorum partem;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L023}{}{}{summam deme de motu latitudinis correcto ad tempus oppositionis; habebis motum latitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L024}{}{}{in initio eclipsis adproximato. Postea minuta illa adde, cum ipsorum duodecima parte, motui}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L025}{25}{}{latitudinis correcto ad tempus oppositionis; exibit generaliter motus latitudinis in fine emer-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L026}{}{}{sionis. Latitudinem Lunae in his duobus temporibus cognosce per motum latitudinis in iisdem;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L027}{}{}{postea quadratum latitudinis lunaris in initio eclipsis deme de quadrato dimidiae summae dua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L028}{}{}{rum diametrorum; residuo adde quadratum differentiae inter latitudines Lunae in initio et in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L029}{}{}{medio eclipsis; radix summae indicat minuta immersionis ab initio ad medium eclipsis. Ea per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L030}{30}{}{praecessionem Lunae (3) divide; exibunt horae, quas de horis oppositionis demes ut horae veri}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L031}{}{}{initii eclipsis remaneant. Postea quadratum latitudinis Lunae in fine emersionis deme de qua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L032}{}{}{drato dimidiae partis duarum diametrorum; residuo adde quadratum differentiae inter latitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L033}{}{}{dines Lunae in medio eclipsis et in fine emersionis; radix summae dat minuta immersionis et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L034}{}{}{morae, quae per praecessionem Lunae divides; quod exibit adde horis oppositionis, et habebis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L035}{35}{}{certo modo horas finis emersionis.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0187-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L036}{}{}{\Indent Similiter si vis tempus initii morae et tempus initii emersionis definire, eadem morae mi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L037}{}{p. 149.}{nuta, quae per praecessionem [Lunae] dividere te supra iussi, cum ipsorum duodecima parte}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L038}{}{}{deme de motu latitudinis tempore oppositionis, et etiam eidem motui adde, ut motum latitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L039}{}{}{dinis utroque [eclipsis] tempore cognoscas. Ab hoc motu, ut vidimus, latitudinem Lunae am-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L040}{40}{}{bobus temporibus disce, \textit{eamque de semisumma duarum diametrorum deme; quadratum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L041}{}{}{\textit{quantitatis, qua residuum superat diametrum lunarem, deme de quadrato minutorum}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-body-L042}{}{}{\textit{totius morae; quod remanet serva. Postea ei adde differentiam} inter latitudines Lunae}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0187-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Praecessio Lunae respectu Solis est motus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-notes_left-L002}{}{}{synodicus horarius.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0187-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Moram totam appellat maximam quantita-}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-notes_right-L001}{}{}{tem morae quae eclipsi centrali respondet; idem est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-notes_right-L002}{}{}{ac differentia semidiametrorum umbrae et Lunae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0187-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0187-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) I. e. per Lunae motum synodicum horarium.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0188}{99}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L001}{}{}{in medio eclipsis et eo tempore; radicem huius summae divide per praecessionem Lunae. Quod}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L002}{}{}{e tempore initii provenit deme ab horis oppositionis; quod ad tempus emersionis est, adde}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L003}{}{}{horis oppositionis; habebis horas initii morae et horas initii emersionis (\textbf{\textit{b}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0188-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L004}{}{}{\Indent Si Luna non deficit tota, atque vis digitos eclipsis computare ut aream disci lunaris in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L005}{5}{}{circulum umbrae incidentem cognoscas, ea ratione qua discus Lunae in 12 partes seu digitos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L006}{}{}{dividitur, de semidiametro aequata Lunae deme 0° 14′ 50″, quae sunt semidiametrus Lunae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L007}{}{}{in apogeo; residuum in sex multiplica et per semidiametrum lunarem in apogeo divide; digitos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L008}{}{}{vel partes digiti provenientes adde sex digitis qui semidiametrum apogeam Lunae, digitis ex-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L009}{}{}{pressam, perficiunt; quod invenies indicabit digitos semidiametri aequatae Lunae. Hos digitos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L010}{10}{}{duplicatos, idest totam diametrum Lunae perficientes, multiplica in 3° 8′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ (1), quae sunt}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L011}{}{}{ratio circumferentiae ad diametrum; productum indicat circumferentiam disci lunaris. Eius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L012}{}{}{dimidiam partem in digitos semidiametri Lunae multiplica; exibit superficies disci lunaris.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L013}{}{}{Postea id quo semidiametrus aequata umbrae excedit 0° 38′ 30″ (2), duplicatum multiplica in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L014}{}{}{12, et per 0° 77′, quae sunt diametrus apogea umbrae, divide; exibunt digiti, quos adde 31 $\qfrac{1}{5}$}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L015}{15}{}{digitis, qui minorem diametrum umbrae in maxima distantia Lunae perficiunt; summa dat}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L016}{}{}{digitos diametri umbrae. Eos multiplica in 3° 8′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$; habebis circumferentiam umbrae, cuius}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L017}{}{}{dimidiam partem in digitos semidiametri umbrae multiplica ut superficiem circuli umbrae in-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L018}{}{}{venias. Postea digitos diametri umbrae et diametri Lunae in unum collige; exeunt digiti dua-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L019}{}{p. 150.}{rum diametrorum, quos serva. Digitos eclipsis multiplica in digitos diametri Lunae, et produ-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L020}{20}{}{ctum per 12 divide; exeunt digiti correcti eclipsis, quos duplicatos deme a digitis duarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L021}{}{}{diametrorum; remanet dupla duorum centrorum distantia.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0188-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L022}{}{}{\Indent Postea digitos correctos eclipsis detrahe a digitis diametri lunaris; residuum in digitos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L023}{}{}{correctos eclipsis multiplica, et productum per duplam centrorum distantiam divide; remanet}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L024}{}{}{sagitta circuli umbrae. Eam de digitis correctis eclipsis deme; residuum erit sagitta disci lu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L025}{25}{}{naris. Hanc de digitis diametri Lunae deme; quod remanet multiplica in sagittam disci lunaris;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L026}{}{}{radix producti erit dimidius sinus communis. — Digitos correctos eclipsis, si minores sunt digitis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L027}{}{}{semidiametri lunaris, de digitis semidiametri Lunae deme; si maiores, ab iis detrahe digitos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L028}{}{}{semidiametri Lunae. Quod a decrescentia provenit, adde sagittae umbrae (3); quod contra ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L029}{}{}{excrescentia oritur, cum sagitta umbrae (4) compara, et differentiam sume. Quae alterutra via}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L030}{30}{}{proveniunt multiplica in dimidium sinum communem; productum erit area trianguli Lunae. —}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L031}{}{}{Item a digitis semidiametri umbrae sagittam umbrae (5) deme; residuum multiplica in dimi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L032}{}{}{dium sinum communem; remanet area trianguli umbrae. — Postea dimidium sinum commu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L033}{}{}{nem in 6 multiplica, et productum per digitos semidiametri Lunae divide; quod exibit multi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L034}{}{}{plica in 10 partes, ut eius portionem e semidiametro habeas (6), et quae erit in arcum per}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L035}{35}{}{tabulam sinuum (7) converte. Arcum inventum multiplica in digitos quadrantis circumferentiae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L036}{}{}{lunaris: habebis portionem arcus, quae, per 90 (8) divisa, dabit arcum Lunae. Hunc mul-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L037}{}{}{tiplica in digitos semidiametri lunaris, et habebis aream arcus Lunae. — Postea dimidium}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L038}{}{}{sinum communem multiplica in 15 $\qfrac{3}{5}$, qui sunt digiti semidiametri minoris umbrae; produ-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-body-L039}{}{}{ctum divide per digitos semidiametri umbrae, et quod provenit multiplica in 3\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 50′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$ +}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0188-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) I. e. 3 $\qfrac{17}{120}$; cfr. cap. III, p. 9, et \textit{Almag.}}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-notes_left-L002}{}{}{VI, 7 (ed. Halma, t. I, p. 421).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0188-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-notes_left-L003}{}{}{\Indent (2) Cod. 0° 18′ 39″; est semidiametrus umbrae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-notes_left-L004}{}{}{in apogeo.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0188-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-notes_left-L005}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cod. et Plato: « disci lunaris ».}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0188-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cod. et Plato: « disci lunaris ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0188-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-notes_right-L002}{}{}{\Indent (5) Cod. et Plato: « disci lunaris ».}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0188-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (6) Nam R = 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}}.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0188-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (7) Fol. 176,v.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0188-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0188-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (8) Plato 40.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0189}{100}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L001}{}{}{+ $\qfrac{1}{5\times 10}$ (1), ut fiat ad semidiametrum proportionalis. Productum in arcum converte, tabula}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L002}{}{}{sinuum utens; arcum multiplica in quartam partem circumferentiae umbrae, et per 90 divide;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L003}{}{}{quotiens erit arcus umbrae, qui, in digitos semidiametri umbrae multiplicatus, aream arcus}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L004}{}{}{umbrae efficit. Hanc adde areae arcus Lunae; de summa deme superficiem trianguli umbrae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L005}{5}{}{simul cum superficie trianguli Lunae collectam; remanet superficies partis occultatae e disco}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L006}{}{}{lunari. Eam in 12 multiplica, et productum per aream disci lunaris antea servatam divide;}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L007}{}{}{digiti exeuntes indicant partem disci Lunae occultatam, iuxta rationem qua tota superficies}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L008}{}{}{disci in 12 digitos dividitur (\textbf{\textit{c}}).}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0189-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L009}{}{p. 151.}{\Indent Si azimuth (2) regionis horizontis in qua immersio vel emersio fiunt, et figuram eclipsis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L010}{10}{}{cognoscere vis, ascendens singulis eclipsis temporibus supputa, et azimuth ascendentium, ut in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L011}{}{}{anteriore parte libri docuimus [cap. VII], inveni. Latitudines Lunae tempore initii eclipsis et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L012}{}{}{finis emersionis accipe, si Luna non deficit tota; si enim Luna tota deficit et cum mora [in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L013}{}{}{umbra], etiam latitudines in initio morae et in initio emersionis sumere debes. Singulas lati-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L014}{}{}{tudines in semidiametrum multiplica; quae ex initio morae et emersionis proveniunt divide}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L015}{15}{}{per minuta totius morae (3); quae ex initio immersionis et fine emersionis exeunt, divide}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L016}{}{}{per dimidiam duarum diametrorum summam; invenies gradus, quos per tabulam sinuum in}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L017}{}{}{arcus converte; arcus singulis temporibus contingens est quantitas inclinationis obscuritatis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L018}{}{}{eclipsis eo tempore. Eam et plagam Caeli in qua est, serva. — Nam si in aliquo eorum tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L019}{}{}{porum centrum verum Lunae in ecliptica est, scilicet latitudine caret, cum id eveniat in initio}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L020}{20}{}{eclipsis et in initio emersionis, initium obscuritatis et initium emersionis erunt utroque tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L021}{}{}{pore a parte azimuth ascendentis; cum autem id in initio morae et in fine emersionis contin-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L022}{}{}{gat, ambo a parte azimuth gradus iis temporibus occidentis erunt. — Si Luna non est in linea}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L023}{}{}{zodiaci, scilicet ab alterutra eclipticae parte latitudinem habet, hoc modo inclinationem ecli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L024}{}{}{psis in initio immersionis et fine (4) emersionis deprehendes: tempore initii immersionis ab}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L025}{25}{}{azimuth ascendentis in initio eclipsis ad plagam contrariam latitudini Lunae [procedens]; tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L026}{}{}{pore finis emersionis ab azimuth eius gradus occidentis ad plagam oppositam latitudini Lunae.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L027}{}{}{In initio emersionis et initio morae, ad inveniendam inclinationem obscuritatis eclipsis ita [pro-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L028}{}{}{cede]: tempore initii emersionis ab azimuth gradus tunc ascendentis in eandem plagam latitu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L029}{}{}{dinis Lunae; tempore initii morae ab azimuth gradus tunc occidentis in plagam latitudinis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L030}{30}{}{lunaris. Gradus amplitudinis ad quem in circulo horizontis numerando iis temporibus perve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L031}{}{}{nies, indicabit inclinationem obscuritatis et emersionis disci lunaris.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0189-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Si Luna non deficit tota, inclinatio obscuritatis in medio eclipsis semper ad angulos rectos}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L033}{}{}{super eclipticam incidit; idque evenit in loco, quem definit arcus per polos eclipticae, locum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L034}{}{}{Lunae et circulum horizontis transiens (5). Ut id cognoscas, angulum longitudinis, qui invenitur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L035}{35}{p. 152.}{ad tempus mediae eclipsis ut ad cognoscendam parallaxim Lunae docuimus [cap. XXXIX],}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L036}{}{}{accipe, [eum numerans] a termino azimuth ascendentis [tempore] mediae eclipsis versus par-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L037}{}{}{tem contrariam latitudini Lunae, si Luna ab oriente est; Luna contra in parte occidentali}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-body-L038}{}{}{exstante, angulum illum collige ab extremitate azimuth gradus occidentis [tempore] medii ecli-}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0189-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Plato tantum $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$ habet; cod.: « dimi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_left-L002}{}{}{« diam partem et quadrantem minuti atque ..... de-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_left-L003}{}{}{« cimae partis minuti ». Verbum tineis corrosum, e}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_left-L004}{}{}{tenuis vestigiis patet $\qfrac{1}{5}$ vel $\qfrac{1}{6}$ fuisse. Primam fra-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_left-L005}{}{}{ctionem delegi, nam 60\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} : 15 $\qfrac{3}{5}$ = 3\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 50′ $\qfrac{120}{156}$ =}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_left-L006}{}{}{3\textsuperscript{\textit{p}} 50′ $\qfrac{1}{2}$ + $\qfrac{1}{4}$ + $\qfrac{1}{52}$.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0189-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (2) Sive amplitudines; Arabes enim azimuth}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_right-L002}{}{}{supputant a linea E-W incipientes.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0189-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (3) Cfr. pag. 98, adn. 2.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0189-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (4) Cod. « initio »; Plato nihil habet.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0189-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (5) Idem loquendi modus alibi quoque inveni-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0189-notes_right-L006}{}{}{tur; cfr. pag. 77, adn. 1.}
\end{minipage}
\DSource{0190}{101}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L001}{}{}{psis in partem contrariam latitudini lunari. Locus horizontis ad quem numerando pervenis, est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L002}{}{}{gradus inclinationis obscuritatis in eclipsis medio. Ita fit si latitudo Lunae est borealis; cum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L003}{}{}{autem sit australis et ab oriente, angulum ab azimuth occidentis, sed si ab occidente, angu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L004}{}{}{lum ab azimuth gradus ascendentis numera in plagam contrariam latitudini Lunae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-P01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L005}{5}{}{\Indent Si modo veritati adproximato vis eclipses Lunae per tabulas cognoscere, latitudinem ve-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L006}{}{}{ram Lunae tempore oppositionis quaere in duabus eclipsium lunarium tabellis (1), quarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L007}{}{}{altera distantiae apogeae altera perigeae assignata est. Si eam tantum in tabella minimae di-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L008}{}{}{stantiae invenis, ex digitis [diametri lunaris] minutisque incidentiae quae latitudini illi respon-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L009}{}{}{dent, sume partem proportionalem ad partem, quae ex 60 minutis sunt minuta portionum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L010}{10}{}{distantiae, descripta in tertia columna tabulae correctionis (2) prope anomaliam Lunae ad tem-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L011}{}{}{pus oppositionis aequatam. Quod ex digitis minutisque provenit est quantitas digitorum ecli-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L012}{}{}{psis (3) et quantitas incidentiae (4). — Si latitudinem Lunae in utraque tabella invenis, ex}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L013}{}{}{utraque sume digitos, incidentiam, moram; differentias inter numeros ambarum tabellarum}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L014}{}{}{inveni, et ex his differentiis sume partem proportionalem ad partem, quae ex 60 sunt minuta}
