\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L015}{15}{}{tertiae columnae in tabula correctionis prope anomaliam Lunae descripta. Numeros ita exeuntes}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L016}{}{}{adde semper numeris similibus antea e prima tabella maximae distantiae sumptis; habebis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L017}{}{}{digitos diametri lunaris occultatos, quantitatem incidentiae et quantitatem morae, si Luna mo-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L018}{}{}{ram [in umbra] facit. Digitis minus quam 12 exstantibus, Luna non deficit tota nec moratur}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L019}{}{}{[in umbra]. Si digiti superant 12, Luna deficit tota, moramque habet iuxta quantitatem qua}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L020}{20}{}{in umbram intrat; si denique numerus digitorum 12 est, Luna deficit tota sed sine mora.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-P02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L021}{}{}{\Indent Postea minuta incidentiae per praecessionem Lunae (5) divide; itemque divide minuta}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L022}{}{}{morae, si Luna moratur; exibunt horae incidentiae et horae morae. Si Luna moram non ducit,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L023}{}{p. 153.}{horas incidentiae deme de horis oppositionis, ut habeas horas initii immersionis; aut adde horis}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L024}{}{}{oppositionis ut horas finis emersionis invenias. — Horae oppositionis sunt horae medii eclipsis.}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L025}{25}{}{— Si contra Luna moratur [in umbra], horas incidentiae et morae in unum collectas deme}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L026}{}{}{de horis oppositionis, ut exeant horae initii immersionis; aut horis oppositionis adde, ut horae}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L027}{}{}{finis emersionis proveniant. Si horas morae tantum de horis oppositionis demis, habes horas}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L028}{}{}{initii morae; aut si horis oppositionis addis, exeunt circiter horae initii emersionis. — Si digiti}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L029}{}{}{occultati sunt minores quam 12, eos in columna numerorum in tabula quantitatum eclipsium (6)}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L030}{30}{}{quaere; quod iis respondet in secunda columna, quantitatibus eclipsium lunarium assignata,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L031}{}{}{est pars disci lunaris deficiens, iuxta rationem qua disci superficies in 12 partes dividitur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-P03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L032}{}{}{\Indent Si a qua parte obscuratio eclipsis incipiat et a qua parte emersio desinat scire cupis,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L033}{}{}{digitos occultatos diametri lunaris quaere in columna complectente numeros digitorum, in ta-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L034}{}{}{bula inclinationis obscuritatis (7); atque sume quod iis respondet in tertia columna, et etiam,}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L035}{35}{}{si Luna moram agit, in quarta. A tertia columna provenit inclinatio in initio immersionis et}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L036}{}{}{fine emersionis; a quarta inclinatio in initio morae et initio emersionis. Considera postea azi-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L037}{}{}{muthorum imaginem (8), in qua septem circulos ad septem climata descripsimus; ibi sume}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-body-L038}{}{}{azimuth notatum sub signo zodiacali ascendenti et occidenti in tuo climate, nec non azimuth}
\par\vspace{6pt}\nointerlineskip\hbox to\textwidth{\hss\vrule width 115.43pt height.25pt depth0pt\hss}\prevdepth=-1000pt \vspace{5pt}
\noindent\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-N01}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_left-L001}{}{}{\Indent (1) Fol. 195,v.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-N02}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_left-L002}{}{}{\Indent (2) Fol. 195,r.; numeri anomaliae (qui sunt ar-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_left-L003}{}{}{gumentum tabulae) duas columnas efficiunt.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-N03}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_left-L004}{}{}{\Indent (3) I. e. digitorum diametri lunaris qui occul-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_left-L005}{}{}{tantur.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-N04}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_left-L006}{}{}{\Indent (4) I. e. arcus quem Luna describit dum obscu-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_left-L007}{}{}{ratio augetur.}
\end{minipage}
\hfill\vrule width.3pt\hfill\begin{minipage}[t]{.48\textwidth}\vspace{0pt}\fontsize{9}{11.5}\selectfont\setlength{\GutterOffset}{0pt}\setlength{\FullSourceWidth}{\linewidth}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-N05}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_right-L001}{}{}{\Indent (5) I. e. motum synodicum (vel relativum) ho-}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_right-L002}{}{}{rarium Lunae.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-N06}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_right-L003}{}{}{\Indent (6) F. 195,r.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-N07}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_right-L004}{}{}{\Indent (7) F. 195,r.}
\hypertarget{AB01-PDF0190-N08}{}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_right-L005}{}{}{\Indent (8) F. 196,v. — Sunt azimuth, quae amplitudines}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_right-L006}{}{}{etiam vocari possunt, nam a linea E-W, ut mos est}
\DLine{AB01-PDF0190-notes_right-L007}{}{}{Arabum, incipiunt.}
